"""
Cybersecurity Decision Support API.

Provides human decision-support reports.

Does NOT:
- execute remediation;
- modify security systems;
- perform autonomous actions.
"""

from fastapi import APIRouter


from cybersecurity.decision_support.service import (
    CybersecurityDecisionSupportService,
)



router = APIRouter(

    prefix="/api/cybersecurity-center",

    tags=[
        "cybersecurity-center"
    ],

)



_service = (
    CybersecurityDecisionSupportService()
)



@router.post(
    "/decision-brief"
)
async def create_decision_brief(
    intelligence: dict,
):

    """
    Generate a cybersecurity decision brief.

    Input:
        Cybersecurity intelligence context.

    Output:
        Human decision-support information.
    """


    result = await _service.create_brief(
        intelligence
    )


    return result

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\api\platform_intelligence_review.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""Operations Center HTTP routes for Platform Intelligence human review."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission


def create_platform_intelligence_review_router(
    *,
    api: PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
) -> APIRouter:
    """Create the read-only PI review router.

    The caller owns composition and dependency injection. No global runtime or
    repository is constructed here.
    """
    if api is None:
        raise ValueError("api is required.")

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence",
        tags=["operations-center", "platform-intelligence"],
    )
    read_dependency = Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))

    @router.get("/reviews", dependencies=[read_dependency])
    def get_reviews() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return api.list_reviews()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    @router.get("/reviews/{review_item_id}", dependencies=[read_dependency])
    def get_review(review_item_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            result = api.get_review(review_item_id)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

        if result is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
                detail="Platform Intelligence review item not found.",
            )
        return result

    @router.get("/escalations", dependencies=[read_dependency])
    def get_escalations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return api.list_escalations()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    return router


__all__ = ["create_platform_intelligence_review_router"]

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcements\approval\composition.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from fastapi import APIRouter

from app.api.announcement_operations_approval import (
    create_announcement_operations_approval_router,
)
from app.application.announcements.approval.async_approval_service import (
    AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AnnouncementApprovalComposition:
    service: AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService
    router: APIRouter


def create_announcement_approval_composition(
    *,
    approval_service: AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
) -> AnnouncementApprovalComposition:
    if approval_service is None:
        raise ValueError("approval_service cannot be None.")

    router = create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
        approval_service=approval_service,
    )

    return AnnouncementApprovalComposition(
        service=approval_service,
        router=router,
    )


__all__ = [
    "AnnouncementApprovalComposition",
    "create_announcement_approval_composition",
]

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcements\execution\approved_publication_execution_composition.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""WS-23.1.4 composition boundary.

Reuses the already-composed WS-22 durable execution service.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.application.announcements.execution.approved_publication_execution import (
    ApprovedPublicationExecutionService,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionService,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ApprovedPublicationExecutionComposition:
    service: ApprovedPublicationExecutionService
    durable_execution: DurablePublicationExecutionService


def create_approved_publication_execution_composition(
    *,
    durable_execution: DurablePublicationExecutionService,
) -> ApprovedPublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Compose WS-23.1.4 without creating any new infrastructure."""

    if durable_execution is None:
        raise ValueError("durable_execution cannot be None.")

    service = ApprovedPublicationExecutionService(
        durable_execution=durable_execution,
    )

    return ApprovedPublicationExecutionComposition(
        service=service,
        durable_execution=durable_execution,
    )


__all__ = [
    "ApprovedPublicationExecutionComposition",
    "create_approved_publication_execution_composition",
]



------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcements\execution\durable_execution_composition.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""WS-22.3.5.3 canonical durable publication execution composition.

This module is the composition boundary for the WS-22 durable execution
vertical slice.

It deliberately does not create a new persistence architecture. The
canonical PostgreSQL engine and async session factory are supplied by the
platform persistence boundary in:

    app.infrastructure.persistence.database

The resulting object graph is:

    PostgreSQL
        |
        v
    canonical async session factory
        |
        +--> execution repository
        |
        +--> audit repository
                  |
                  v
        DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
                  |
                  v
        DurablePublicationExecutionService

Provider selection remains outside this module.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine, AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.announcements.execution.audit_adapter import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionAudit,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionService,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.publication_audit_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Fully composed WS-22 durable execution boundary."""

    engine: AsyncEngine
    session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]
    execution_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository
    audit_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository
    audit: DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
    service: DurablePublicationExecutionService

    async def dispose(self) -> None:
        """Release the shared PostgreSQL async engine."""

        await self.engine.dispose()


def create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
    *,
    publisher,
) -> DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Create the canonical WS-22 durable execution graph.

    The publisher is deliberately injected because provider selection belongs
    outside the durable execution composition.

    This function creates exactly one PostgreSQL engine and exactly one async
    session factory. Both durable repositories receive that same factory.
    """

    if publisher is None:
        raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    session_factory = create_async_session_factory(engine)

    execution_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository(
        session_factory
    )

    audit_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository(
        session_factory
    )

    audit = DurablePublicationExecutionAudit(
        repository=audit_repository,
    )

    service = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
        publisher=publisher,
        execution_repository=execution_repository,
        audit=audit,
    )

    return DurablePublicationExecutionComposition(
        engine=engine,
        session_factory=session_factory,
        execution_repository=execution_repository,
        audit_repository=audit_repository,
        audit=audit,
        service=service,
    )


__all__ = [
    "DurablePublicationExecutionComposition",
    "create_durable_publication_execution_composition",
]

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

from app.ai_runtime.conversation.conversation_runtime import (
    ConversationRuntime,
)
from app.application.beta_access.beta_access_service import (
    BetaAccessService,
)

    BetaTelegramConversationBoundary,
)
from app.application.beta_access.policies import (
    InMemoryTelegramAbuseProtector,
    InMemoryTelegramRateLimiter,
    LoggingTelegramObservability,
)
from app.application.beta_access.telegram_conversation_service import (
    TelegramConversationService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.functionality_operational_control_service import (
    FunctionalityOperationalControlService,
)
from app.application.private_chat.telegram_private_chat_bridge import (
    TelegramPrivateChatBridge,
)

    ProviderUsageCapture,
    ProviderUsageContext,
)

    BetaWorldAccessService,
)

    LanguageDetector,
)

    LanguageResponsePolicy,
)

from app.application.world_telegram.world_telegram_start_router import (
    WorldTelegramStartRouter,
)

    BetaPrivateChatGate,
)

    BetaTelegramStartHandler,
)

    BetaTelegramAdapter,

    TelegramBotRuntime,

    YamlWorldRepository,
)

    InMemoryProviderUsageRepository,
    ProviderUsageRepository,
)

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BetaTelegramComposition:
    beta_access_service: BetaAccessService
    beta_world_access_service: BetaWorldAccessService
    conversation_runtime: ConversationRuntime
    conversation_service: TelegramConversationService
    conversation_boundary: BetaTelegramConversationBoundary
    private_chat_bridge: TelegramPrivateChatBridge | None
    private_chat_gate: BetaPrivateChatGate
    start_handler: BetaTelegramStartHandler
    adapter: BetaTelegramAdapter
    runtime: TelegramBotRuntime
    rate_limiter: Any | None = None
    abuse_protector: Any | None = None
    observability: Any | None = None
    provider_usage_repository: ProviderUsageRepository | None = None


def _env_positive_int(name: str, default: int) -> int:
    raw = os.getenv(name)

    if raw is None:
        return default

    try:
        value = int(raw)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{name} must be a positive integer."
        ) from exc

    if value <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{name} must be a positive integer."
        )

    return value


def _env_positive_float(name: str, default: float) -> float:
    raw = os.getenv(name)

    if raw is None:
        return default

    try:
        value = float(raw)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{name} must be a positive number."
        ) from exc

    if value <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{name} must be a positive number."
        )

    return value


def create_beta_telegram_composition(
    *,
    user_repository: Any,
    token_repository: Any,
    binding_repository: Any,
    conversation_event_repository: Any,
    privacy_identifier_service: Any,
    conversation_event_validator: Any,
    beta_world_access_service: BetaWorldAccessService,
    expiration_minutes: int = 1440,
    conversation_runtime: ConversationRuntime | None = None,
    conversation_runtime_factory: (
        Callable[[], ConversationRuntime] | None
    ) = None,
    rate_limiter: Any | None = None,
    abuse_protector: Any | None = None,
    observability: Any | None = None,
    provider_usage_capture: ProviderUsageCapture | None = None,
    provider_usage_repository: ProviderUsageRepository | None = None,
    functionality_control_service: (
        FunctionalityOperationalControlService | None
    ) = None,
    world_resolver: WorldResolver | None = None,
    language_response_policy: LanguageResponsePolicy | None = None,
    language_detector: LanguageDetector | None = None,
    private_chat_service_factory: Callable[[], Any] | None = None,
    world_start_router: WorldTelegramStartRouter | None = None,
) -> BetaTelegramComposition:
    """Create the Beta Telegram application composition.

    BetaAccessService owns Telegram identity binding.

    BetaWorldAccessService owns durable provisioning of the complete
    Learning World portfolio after successful Beta onboarding.

    Telegram never selects or changes the user's active World.
    """

    if expiration_minutes <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            "expiration_minutes must be greater than zero."
        )

    if beta_world_access_service is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "beta_world_access_service is required."
        )

    if (
        conversation_runtime is not None
        and conversation_runtime_factory is not None
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Provide either conversation_runtime or "
            "conversation_runtime_factory, not both."
        )

    # Application configuration root:
    #
    # backend/app
    #
    app_root = (
        Path(__file__)
        .resolve()
        .parents[2]
    )

    world_repository = YamlWorldRepository(
        worlds_path=(
            app_root
            / "configuration"
            / "worlds"
        )
    )

    if world_resolver is None:
        world_resolver = WorldResolver(
            world_repository=world_repository,
        )

    beta_access_service = BetaAccessService(
        user_repository=user_repository,
        token_repository=token_repository,
        binding_repository=binding_repository,
        world_resolver=world_resolver,
        expiration_minutes=expiration_minutes,
    )

    resolved_usage_repository = provider_usage_repository

    if conversation_runtime is not None:
        resolved_conversation_runtime = conversation_runtime

    elif conversation_runtime_factory is not None:
        resolved_conversation_runtime = conversation_runtime_factory()

        # P1.6.1: when production supplies a durable usage repository,
        # attach usage capture to the factory-created ConversationRuntime.
        # Keep the existing factory API and explicit runtime injection seam.
        if (
            provider_usage_repository is not None
            and provider_usage_capture is None
            and isinstance(
                resolved_conversation_runtime,
                ConversationRuntime,
            )
            and resolved_conversation_runtime.usage_capture is None
        ):
            provider_usage_service = ProviderUsageService(
                provider_usage_repository
            )

            provider_usage_capture = ProviderUsageCapture(
                provider_usage_service
            )

            resolved_conversation_runtime = replace(
                resolved_conversation_runtime,
                usage_capture=provider_usage_capture,
                usage_context=ProviderUsageContext(
                    capability="conversation",
                    functionality="chat",
                    agent="conversation_agent",
                ),
            )

    else:
        if provider_usage_capture is None:
            if resolved_usage_repository is None:
                resolved_usage_repository = (
                    InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
                )

            provider_usage_service = ProviderUsageService(
                resolved_usage_repository
            )

            provider_usage_capture = ProviderUsageCapture(
                provider_usage_service
            )

        resolved_conversation_runtime = ConversationRuntime(
            llm_provider=LLMProviderFactory.create(),
            usage_capture=provider_usage_capture,
            usage_context=ProviderUsageContext(
                capability="conversation",
                functionality="chat",
                agent="conversation_agent",
            ),
        )

    resolved_language_response_policy = (
        language_response_policy
        or LanguageResponsePolicy()
    )

    if language_detector is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "language_detector is required for "
            "Beta Telegram composition."
        )

    conversation_service = TelegramConversationService(
        conversation_runtime=resolved_conversation_runtime,
        conversation_event_repository=conversation_event_repository,
        privacy_identifier_service=privacy_identifier_service,
        conversation_event_validator=conversation_event_validator,
        language_response_policy=resolved_language_response_policy,
        language_detector=language_detector,
        world_resolver=world_resolver,
    )

    resolved_rate_limiter = (
        rate_limiter
        or InMemoryTelegramRateLimiter(
            max_messages=_env_positive_int(
                "TELEGRAM_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_MESSAGES",
                100,
            ),
            window_seconds=_env_positive_float(
                "TELEGRAM_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS",
                86400.0,
            ),
        )
    )

    resolved_abuse_protector = (
        abuse_protector
        or InMemoryTelegramAbuseProtector(
            max_messages=_env_positive_int(
                "TELEGRAM_ABUSE_MAX_MESSAGES",
                10,
            ),
            window_seconds=_env_positive_float(
                "TELEGRAM_ABUSE_WINDOW_SECONDS",
                60.0,
            ),
        )
    )

    resolved_observability = (
        observability
        or LoggingTelegramObservability(
            privacy_identifier_service=privacy_identifier_service,
        )
    )

    private_chat_bridge = None

    if private_chat_service_factory is not None:
        private_chat_bridge = TelegramPrivateChatBridge(
            private_chat_service_factory=private_chat_service_factory,
        )

    conversation_boundary = BetaTelegramConversationBoundary(
        conversation_service=conversation_service,
        beta_access_service=beta_access_service,
        rate_limiter=resolved_rate_limiter,
        abuse_protector=resolved_abuse_protector,
        observability=resolved_observability,
        functionality_control_service=functionality_control_service,
        private_chat_bridge=private_chat_bridge,
    )

    private_chat_gate = BetaPrivateChatGate(
        beta_access_service=beta_access_service,
    )

    start_handler = BetaTelegramStartHandler(
    beta_access_service=beta_access_service,
    beta_world_access_service=beta_world_access_service,
    privacy_identifier_service=privacy_identifier_service,
)

    adapter = BetaTelegramAdapter(
        start_handler=start_handler,
        private_chat_gate=private_chat_gate,
        conversation_boundary=conversation_boundary,
        world_start_router=world_start_router,
    )

    runtime = TelegramBotRuntime(
        adapter=adapter,
        conversation_boundary=conversation_boundary,
    )

    return BetaTelegramComposition(
        beta_access_service=beta_access_service,
        beta_world_access_service=beta_world_access_service,
        conversation_runtime=resolved_conversation_runtime,
        conversation_service=conversation_service,
        conversation_boundary=conversation_boundary,
        private_chat_bridge=private_chat_bridge,
        private_chat_gate=private_chat_gate,
        start_handler=start_handler,
        adapter=adapter,
        runtime=runtime,
        rate_limiter=resolved_rate_limiter,
        abuse_protector=resolved_abuse_protector,
        observability=resolved_observability,
        provider_usage_repository=resolved_usage_repository,
    )


__all__ = [
    "BetaTelegramComposition",
    "create_beta_telegram_composition",
]

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\composition\learner_context_composition.py

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from app.application.conversation.conversation_event_repository import (
    ConversationEventRepository,
)
from app.application.learner_context.postgresql_repository import (
    CrossChannelLearnerContextRepository,
)
from app.application.learner_context.service import LearnerContextService
from app.application.memory.memory_repository import (
    ConversationMemoryRepository,
)
from app.application.privacy.privacy_identifier_service import (
    PrivacyIdentifierService,
)
from app.application.uai.world.world_resolver import WorldResolver
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_beta_access_repository import (
    PostgreSQLTelegramBindingRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_conversation_event_repository import (
    PostgreSQLConversationEventRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_memory_repository import (
    PostgreSQLConversationMemoryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.worlds.yaml_world_repository import YamlWorldRepository


def build_learner_context_service(
    *,
    telegram_bindings,
    memory_repository: ConversationMemoryRepository,
    conversation_event_repository: ConversationEventRepository,
    privacy_identifier_service: PrivacyIdentifierService,
    world_resolver: WorldResolver,
) -> LearnerContextService:
    """Compose the C4.2 cross-channel learner-context service.

    All dependencies are injected. This function creates no database
    connection and no persistence implementation.
    """
    repository = CrossChannelLearnerContextRepository(
        telegram_bindings=telegram_bindings,
        memory_repository=memory_repository,
        conversation_event_repository=conversation_event_repository,
        privacy_identifier_service=privacy_identifier_service,
        world_resolver=world_resolver,
    )

    return LearnerContextService(repository=repository)


def build_production_learner_context_service(
    *,
    session_factory: Any,
    world_resolver: WorldResolver | None = None,
    telegram_binding_repository=None,
    memory_repository: ConversationMemoryRepository | None = None,
    conversation_event_repository: ConversationEventRepository | None = None,
    privacy_identifier_service: PrivacyIdentifierService | None = None,
    world_repository_factory: Callable[[], Any] | None = None,
) -> LearnerContextService:
    """Build the production C4.2 service from existing infrastructure.

    The default path creates repositories against the supplied existing
    synchronous session factory. No new database engine is created here.

    Optional dependency arguments are exposed so application composition
    tests and future composition roots can inject already-created instances.
    """
    if session_factory is None:
        raise ValueError("session_factory is required.")

    if telegram_binding_repository is None:
        telegram_binding_repository = PostgreSQLTelegramBindingRepository(
            session_factory,
        )

    if memory_repository is None:
        memory_repository = PostgreSQLConversationMemoryRepository(
            session_factory,
        )

    if conversation_event_repository is None:
        conversation_event_repository = PostgreSQLConversationEventRepository(
            session_factory,
        )

    if privacy_identifier_service is None:
        privacy_identifier_service = PrivacyIdentifierService()

    if world_resolver is None:
        if world_repository_factory is not None:
            world_repository = world_repository_factory()
        else:
            app_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
            world_repository = YamlWorldRepository(
                worlds_path=app_root / "configuration" / "worlds",
            )

        world_resolver = WorldResolver(
            world_repository=world_repository,
        )

    return build_learner_context_service(
        telegram_bindings=telegram_binding_repository,
        memory_repository=memory_repository,
        conversation_event_repository=conversation_event_repository,
        privacy_identifier_service=privacy_identifier_service,
        world_resolver=world_resolver,
    )


__all__ = [
    "build_learner_context_service",
    "build_production_learner_context_service",
]

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\composition\private_chat_composition.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.ai_runtime.conversation.conversation_runtime import ConversationRuntime
from app.application.private_chat.service import PrivateChatService
from app.application.uai.language.language_detector import LanguageDetector
from app.application.uai.language.language_response_policy import (
    LanguageResponsePolicy,
)
from app.application.uai.world.world_resolver import WorldResolver
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_private_chat_repository import (
    PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.worlds.yaml_world_repository import YamlWorldRepository


def build_private_chat_service_factory(
    *,
    session_factory: sessionmaker[Session],
    conversation_runtime: ConversationRuntime,
    character_instruction: str,
    language_detector: LanguageDetector | None = None,
) -> Callable[[], PrivateChatService]:
    """Build a request-scoped private-chat service factory.

    Every invocation creates exactly one SQLAlchemy Session. The returned
    service closes that session through its repository when the HTTP request
    finishes. No database session is shared between requests.
    """

    if session_factory is None:
        raise ValueError("session_factory is required.")
    if conversation_runtime is None:
        raise ValueError("conversation_runtime is required.")
    if not character_instruction or not character_instruction.strip():
        raise ValueError("character_instruction is required.")

    app_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    world_repository = YamlWorldRepository(
        worlds_path=app_root / "configuration" / "worlds",
    )
    world_resolver = WorldResolver(
        world_repository=world_repository,
    )
    language_response_policy = LanguageResponsePolicy()

    def factory() -> PrivateChatService:
        session = session_factory()
        repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

        return PrivateChatService(
            repository=repository,
            world_resolver=world_resolver,
            language_response_policy=language_response_policy,
            language_detector=language_detector,
            conversation_runtime=conversation_runtime,
            character_instruction=character_instruction,
        )

    return factory

"""
Voice Call Acceptance Composition.

Composition root for the voice call
acceptance workflow.

Responsibilities:
- Build acceptance dependencies.
- Wire voice services.
- Wire notification services.
- Wire voice event services.
- Wire canonical voice execution.
- Reuse shared application repositories.
- Return configured workflow service.

Does NOT:
- Handle HTTP.
- Send notifications.
- Execute voice runtime directly.
- Manage providers directly.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.composition.voice_execution_coordinator_composition import (
    create_voice_call_execution_coordinator,
)

from app.application.composition.voice_call_dependencies import (
    voice_call_repository,
    voice_call_invitation_repository,
    voice_call_notification_repository,
)

from app.application.composition.voice_event_composition import (
    build_voice_call_event_service,
)

from app.application.uai.notifications.voice_call_notification_service import (
    VoiceCallNotificationService,
)

from app.application.uai.voice.voice_call_acceptance_service import (
    VoiceCallAcceptanceService,
)

from app.application.uai.voice.voice_call_context_factory import (
    VoiceCallContextFactory,
)

from app.application.uai.voice.voice_call_event_service import (
    VoiceCallEventService,
)

from app.application.uai.voice.voice_call_invitation_service import (
    VoiceCallInvitationService,
)

from app.application.uai.voice.voice_call_management_service import (
    VoiceCallManagementService,
)

from app.application.uai.voice.voice_call_session_service import (
    VoiceCallSessionService,
)


def create_voice_call_acceptance_service(
) -> VoiceCallAcceptanceService:
    """
    Create configured acceptance workflow.

    Shared repositories ensure trigger,
    acceptance and reconnect flows
    operate on the same application state.
    """

    voice_call_management_service = (
        VoiceCallManagementService(
            repository=voice_call_repository,
        )
    )

    voice_call_invitation_service = (
        VoiceCallInvitationService(
            repository=voice_call_invitation_repository,
        )
    )

    voice_call_notification_service = (
        VoiceCallNotificationService(
            repository=voice_call_notification_repository,
        )
    )

    voice_call_event_service = (
        build_voice_call_event_service()
    )

    voice_call_context_factory = (
        VoiceCallContextFactory()
    )

    voice_call_execution_coordinator = (
        create_voice_call_execution_coordinator()
    )

    return VoiceCallAcceptanceService(
        voice_call_management_service=(
            voice_call_management_service
        ),
        voice_call_invitation_service=(
            voice_call_invitation_service
        ),
        voice_call_notification_service=(
            voice_call_notification_service
        ),
        voice_call_session_service=(
            VoiceCallSessionService()
        ),
        voice_call_event_service=(
            voice_call_event_service
        ),
        voice_call_context_factory=(

    FunctionalityOperationalControlService,
)

    VoiceCharacterSessionService,
)

    VoiceCallExecutionService,
)

    VoiceSpeechRuntime,

    VoiceCallExecutionRuntime,

    SpeechProviderFactory,

    SpeechProviderConfig,
)

    LiveKitAdapter,
)


def create_voice_character_session_service(
    functionality_control_service: (
        FunctionalityOperationalControlService | None
    ) = None,
) -> VoiceCharacterSessionService:
    """
    Build voice session application service.

    The operational-control service is injected from
    the application composition root when available.
    """

    provider_config = SpeechProviderConfig(
        provider_name="openai",
        model_name="gpt-4o-mini-tts",
        default_language="fr",
    )

    speech_provider = SpeechProviderFactory.create(
        provider_config
    )

    speech_runtime = VoiceSpeechRuntime(
        speech_provider=speech_provider,
        functionality_control_service=functionality_control_service,
    )

    livekit_adapter = LiveKitAdapter()

    return VoiceCharacterSessionService(
        livekit_adapter=livekit_adapter,
        speech_runtime=speech_runtime,
    )


def create_voice_call_execution_service(
) -> VoiceCallExecutionService:
    """
    Build voice call execution service.
    """

    execution_runtime = VoiceCallExecutionRuntime()

    return VoiceCallExecutionService(
        execution_runtime=execution_runtime,
    )

------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\health\dependency_checks.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from typing import Any


from .health_result import (
    HealthCheckResult,
    HealthStatus,
)


Probe = Callable[[], Awaitable[bool | HealthCheckResult]]


class AsyncDependencyCheck:
    """
    Asynchronous production dependency health check.

    The probe is deliberately isolated from the readiness service.

    A probe may return:

        True
            Dependency is healthy.

        False
            Dependency is unhealthy.

        HealthCheckResult
            Caller-provided sanitized health result.

    Exceptions are contained and converted into a generic
    dependency failure result. Internal exception details,
    credentials, connection strings, and secrets are never
    exposed through the result.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        name: str,
        probe: Probe,
    ) -> None:
        if not name or not name.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Dependency check name cannot be empty."
            )

        if not callable(probe):
            raise TypeError(
                "Dependency check probe must be callable."
            )

        self.name = name.strip()
        self.probe = probe

    async def check(self) -> HealthCheckResult:
        """
        Execute the dependency probe safely.
        """

        try:
            result = await self.probe()

        except Exception:
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
                details={
                    "reason": "dependency_check_failed",
                },
            )

        if isinstance(
            result,
            HealthCheckResult,
        ):
            # Ensure the dependency name remains authoritative.
            if result.name != self.name:
                return HealthCheckResult(
                    name=self.name,
                    status=result.status,
                    details=dict(result.details),
                )

            return result

        if result is True:
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            )

        if result is False:
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
                details={
                    "reason": "dependency_unhealthy",
                },
            )

        return HealthCheckResult(
            name=self.name,
            status=HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
            details={
                "reason": "invalid_probe_result",
            },
        )


class ConfigurationCheck:
    """
    Synchronous configuration/readiness check.

    This check verifies whether a required dependency configuration
    exists without exposing the configuration value itself.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        name: str,
        configured: bool,
    ) -> None:
        if not name or not name.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Configuration check name cannot be empty."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            configured,
            bool,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "configured must be a boolean."
            )

        self.name = name.strip()
        self.configured = configured

    async def check(self) -> HealthCheckResult:
        """
        Return a sanitized configuration health result.
        """

        if self.configured:
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.HEALTHY,
                details={
                    "configured": True,
                },
            )

        return HealthCheckResult(
            name=self.name,
            status=HealthStatus.NOT_CONFIGURED,
            details={
                "configured": False,
            },
        )


# Compatibility contract used by WS-21.6 / WS-21.7.
#
# Keep this alias rather than creating another independent abstraction.
HealthDependencyCheck = AsyncDependencyCheck


__all__ = [
    "AsyncDependencyCheck",
    "ConfigurationCheck",
    "HealthDependencyCheck",
]

------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\learning_assessment\questionnaire_runtime_composition.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.application.learning_assessment.assessment_service import (
    AssessmentService,
)
from app.application.learning_assessment.learner_skill_state_service import (
    LearnerSkillStateService,
)
from app.application.learning_assessment.questionnaire_runtime import (
    QuestionnaireRuntime,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_learner_skill_state_repository import (
    PostgreSQLLearnerSkillStateRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_learning_assessment_repositories import (
    PostgreSQLAssessmentAttemptRepository,
    PostgreSQLAssessmentQuestionRepository,
    PostgreSQLLearningEventRepository,
)


def create_questionnaire_runtime(
    *,
    session_factory: sessionmaker[Session],
) -> QuestionnaireRuntime:
    """Compose the production questionnaire runtime from existing boundaries.

    The runtime deliberately reuses the existing synchronous persistence
    session factory and AssessmentService. No second engine or persistence
    boundary is created here.
    """
    if session_factory is None:
        raise ValueError("session_factory is required.")

    question_repository = PostgreSQLAssessmentQuestionRepository(
        session_factory
    )
    attempt_repository = PostgreSQLAssessmentAttemptRepository(
        session_factory
    )
    learning_event_repository = PostgreSQLLearningEventRepository(
        session_factory
    )
    skill_state_repository = PostgreSQLLearnerSkillStateRepository(
        session_factory
    )

    assessment_service = AssessmentService(
        questions=question_repository,
        attempts=attempt_repository,
        events=learning_event_repository,
        skill_states=skill_state_repository,
        skill_state_service=LearnerSkillStateService(),
    )

    return QuestionnaireRuntime(
        assessment_service=assessment_service,
    )


__all__ = ["create_questionnaire_runtime"]

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from .domain import (
    EmailWorkflowDefinition,
    EmailWorkflowLifecycle,
    EmailWorkflowType,
)
from .registry import EmailWorkflowRegistry


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BootstrapResult:
    created: tuple[str, ...]
    already_present: tuple[str, ...]


BUILT_IN_EMAIL_WORKFLOWS: tuple[dict[str, object], ...] = (
    {
        "workflow_id": "cybersecurity_daily_report",
        "name": "Cybersecurity Daily Report",
        "description": "Scheduled daily cybersecurity report email workflow.",
        "workflow_type": EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED,
        "lifecycle": EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
        "owner": "cybersecurity",
        "trigger": "scheduled",
        "schedule": "daily_08:00",
        "template_id": "cybersecurity_daily_report",
    },
    {
        "workflow_id": "cybersecurity_weekly_report",
        "name": "Cybersecurity Weekly Report",
        "description": "Scheduled weekly cybersecurity report email workflow.",
        "workflow_type": EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED,
        "lifecycle": EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
        "owner": "cybersecurity",
        "trigger": "scheduled",
        "schedule": "weekly_monday_08:00",
        "template_id": "cybersecurity_weekly_report",
    },
    {
        "workflow_id": "cybersecurity_critical_alert",
        "name": "Cybersecurity Critical Alert",
        "description": "Event-driven critical cybersecurity alert email workflow.",
        "workflow_type": EmailWorkflowType.EVENT_DRIVEN,
        "lifecycle": EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
        "owner": "cybersecurity",
        "trigger": "critical_event",
        "schedule": None,
        "template_id": "cybersecurity_critical_alert",
    },
    {
        "workflow_id": "platform_intelligence_escalation",
        "name": "Platform Intelligence Escalation",
        "description": (
            "Event-driven human-review escalation notification for "
            "governed Platform Intelligence recommendations."
        ),
        "workflow_type": EmailWorkflowType.EVENT_DRIVEN,
        "lifecycle": EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
        "owner": "platform_intelligence",
        "trigger": "escalation_recommendation",
        "schedule": None,
        "template_id": "platform_intelligence_escalation",
    },
)


class EmailWorkflowRegistryBootstrap:
    def __init__(self, registry: EmailWorkflowRegistry) -> None:
        if registry is None:
            raise ValueError("registry is required.")
        self._registry = registry

    def ensure_registered(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> BootstrapResult:
        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        if current.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError("now must be timezone-aware.")

        created: list[str] = []
        already_present: list[str] = []

        for raw in BUILT_IN_EMAIL_WORKFLOWS:
            workflow_id = str(raw["workflow_id"])

            if self._registry.get(workflow_id) is not None:
                already_present.append(workflow_id)
                continue

            workflow = EmailWorkflowDefinition(
                workflow_id=workflow_id,
                name=str(raw["name"]),
                description=str(raw["description"]),
                workflow_type=raw["workflow_type"],  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                lifecycle=raw["lifecycle"],  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                owner=str(raw["owner"]),
                trigger=str(raw["trigger"]),
                schedule=(
                    str(raw["schedule"])
                    if raw["schedule"] is not None
                    else None
                ),
                template_id=str(raw["template_id"]),
                version=1,
                created_at=current,
                updated_at=current,
            )

            self._registry.create(workflow)
            created.append(workflow_id)

        return BootstrapResult(
            created=tuple(created),
            already_present=tuple(already_present),
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import FrozenSet

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus(StrEnum):
    DRAFT = "DRAFT"
    APPROVED = "APPROVED"


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision(StrEnum):
    ELIGIBLE = "ELIGIBLE"
    NOT_ELIGIBLE = "NOT_ELIGIBLE"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy:
    """Versioned, explicitly approved retry policy for PI escalation delivery."""

    policy_id: str
    policy_version: str
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
    max_attempts: int
    base_backoff_seconds: int
    max_backoff_seconds: int
    retryable_error_codes: FrozenSet[str]
    retry_interrupted_started: bool = True

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        required = (self.policy_id, self.policy_version)

        if any(
            not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip()
            for value in required
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "policy_id and policy_version cannot be blank."
            )

        if self.max_attempts < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_attempts must be at least 1."
            )

        if self.base_backoff_seconds < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "base_backoff_seconds cannot be negative."
            )

        if self.max_backoff_seconds < self.base_backoff_seconds:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_backoff_seconds cannot be lower than "
                "base_backoff_seconds."
            )

        if any(
            not isinstance(code, str) or not code.strip()
            for code in self.retryable_error_codes
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "retryable_error_codes cannot contain blank values."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult:
    """Deterministic retry eligibility decision and policy-derived timing."""

    decision: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
    notification_key: str
    attempt: int
    policy_id: str
    policy_version: str
    reason: str
    next_eligible_at: datetime | None = None
    backoff_seconds: int | None = None


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService:
    """Evaluate whether a durable PI delivery record may be retried.

    This service is side-effect free.

    It does not:
    - mutate delivery state;
    - call an email provider;
    - schedule work;
    - read the wall clock.

    The caller supplies the reference time used for the eligibility decision.
    The delivery record supplies the authoritative timestamp from which the
    retry backoff is calculated.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        policy: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    ) -> None:
        if policy is None:
            raise ValueError("policy cannot be None.")

        self._policy = policy

    @property
    def policy(self) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy:
        return self._policy

    def evaluate(
        self,
        *,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult:
        self._validate_reference_time(reference_at)

        if not record.notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "notification_key cannot be blank."
            )

        if record.attempt < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "delivery attempt must be at least 1."
            )

        if (
            self._policy.status
            is not PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus.APPROVED
        ):
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason="Retry policy is not APPROVED.",
            )

        if (
            record.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        ):
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason="Delivery already SUCCEEDED.",
            )

        if record.attempt >= self._policy.max_attempts:
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason="Maximum delivery attempts reached.",
            )

        if (
            record.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        ):
            if not record.error_code:
                return self._result(
                    record=record,
                    decision=(
                        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                    ),
                    reason=(
                        "FAILED delivery has no error code for retry "
                        "classification."
                    ),
                )

            if (
                record.error_code
                not in self._policy.retryable_error_codes
            ):
                return self._result(
                    record=record,
                    decision=(
                        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                    ),
                    reason=(
                        f"Error code {record.error_code!r} "
                        "is not retryable."
                    ),
                )

            return self._eligible_result(
                record=record,
                reference_at=reference_at,
            )

        if (
            record.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        ):
            if not self._policy.retry_interrupted_started:
                return self._result(
                    record=record,
                    decision=(
                        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                    ),
                    reason=(
                        "Interrupted STARTED deliveries are not "
                        "retryable by policy."
                    ),
                )

            # STARTED represents recovery of an interrupted attempt.
            # Recovery does NOT increment the attempt, but it remains
            # subject to the same policy-controlled backoff.
            return self._eligible_result(
                record=record,
                reference_at=reference_at,
            )

        return self._result(
            record=record,
            decision=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                .NOT_ELIGIBLE
            ),
            reason=(
                f"Delivery status {record.status.value!r} "
                "is not retryable."
            ),
        )

    def backoff_seconds(self, *, attempt: int) -> int:
        """Return deterministic exponential backoff for the retry.

        Attempt 1:
            base_backoff_seconds

        Attempt 2:
            base_backoff_seconds * 2

        Attempt 3:
            base_backoff_seconds * 4

        The result is capped at max_backoff_seconds.
        """
        if attempt < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "attempt must be at least 1."
            )

        multiplier = 2 ** (attempt - 1)

        return min(
            self._policy.base_backoff_seconds * multiplier,
            self._policy.max_backoff_seconds,
        )

    def _eligible_result(
        self,
        *,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult:
        delay = self.backoff_seconds(
            attempt=record.attempt
        )

        # The backoff is calculated from the persisted delivery timestamp,
        # not from the caller's current reference time.
        #
        # This is essential for deterministic due/not-due evaluation.
        if record.updated_at is None:
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason=(
                    "Delivery record has no updated_at timestamp "
                    "from which retry timing can be established."
                ),
            )

        self._validate_timestamp(
            record.updated_at,
            field_name="record.updated_at",
        )

        next_eligible_at = (
            record.updated_at
            + timedelta(seconds=delay)
        )

        if reference_at < next_eligible_at:
            return self._result(
                record=record,
                decision=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                    .ELIGIBLE
                ),
                reason=(
                    "Delivery is eligible but the policy-derived "
                    "backoff window has not elapsed."
                ),
                next_eligible_at=next_eligible_at,
                backoff_seconds=delay,
            )

        return self._result(
            record=record,
            decision=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision
                .ELIGIBLE
            ),
            reason=(
                "Delivery is eligible for a policy-controlled retry."
            ),
            next_eligible_at=next_eligible_at,
            backoff_seconds=delay,
        )

    def _result(
        self,
        *,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
        decision: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision,
        reason: str,
        next_eligible_at: datetime | None = None,
        backoff_seconds: int | None = None,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult:
        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryResult(
            decision=decision,
            notification_key=record.notification_key,
            attempt=record.attempt,
            policy_id=self._policy.policy_id,
            policy_version=self._policy.policy_version,
            reason=reason,
            next_eligible_at=next_eligible_at,
            backoff_seconds=backoff_seconds,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_reference_time(
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        if (
            reference_at.tzinfo is None
            or reference_at.utcoffset() is None
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "reference_at must be timezone-aware."
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_timestamp(
        value: datetime,
        *,
        field_name: str,
    ) -> None:
        if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"{field_name} must be timezone-aware."
            )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidate_discovery.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidates import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateRepository,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService:
    """Discover durable retry work without executing it.

    This boundary deliberately does not:
    - call the retry policy;
    - mutate delivery state;
    - send email;
    - schedule work;
    - claim/lock candidates.

    Policy eligibility and execution remain separate steps.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        repository: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateRepository,
    ) -> None:
        if repository is None:
            raise ValueError("repository cannot be None.")

        self._repository = repository

    async def discover(
        self,
        *,
        limit: int = 100,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch:
        if limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("limit must be at least 1.")

        records = await self._repository.list_retry_candidates(
            limit=limit,
        )

        candidates: list[PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate] = []

        for record in records:
            if record.status not in (
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
            ):
                continue

            candidates.append(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate(
                    notification_key=record.notification_key,
                    recommendation_id=record.recommendation_id,
                    risk_decision_id=record.risk_decision_id,
                    review_item_id=record.review_item_id,
                    correlation_id=record.correlation_id,
                    attempt=record.attempt,
                    status=record.status.value,
                    error_code=record.error_code,
                    updated_at=record.updated_at,
                )
            )

        candidates.sort(
            key=lambda candidate: (
                candidate.updated_at is None,
                candidate.updated_at,
                candidate.notification_key,
            )
        )

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch(
            candidates=tuple(candidates),
            limit=limit,
        )

------------------------------------------------------------

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (

        self,
        *,
        limit: int,
    ) -> Sequence[PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord]:
        """Return durable FAILED/STARTED delivery records for policy evaluation."""
        ...


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate:

    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: str
    error_code: str | None
    updated_at: datetime | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch:

    candidates: tuple[PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate, ...]

"""Application service for controlled retry candidate claiming."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Protocol
from uuid import uuid4

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claims import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaim,
    RetryCandidateClaimStatus,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimRepository(Protocol):
    async def claim_retry_candidate(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        claim_token: str,
        claimed_at: datetime,
        lease_expires_at: datetime,
    ) -> bool: ...


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService:
    """Claims a candidate for one bounded execution attempt.

    Claiming is coordination only. It does not evaluate retry policy, send
    email, mutate the delivery lifecycle beyond the lease, or authorize the
    escalation itself.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimRepository,
        *,
        lease_seconds: int = 300,
    ) -> None:
        if lease_seconds < 1:
            raise ValueError("lease_seconds must be at least 1.")
        self._repository = repository
        self._lease_seconds = lease_seconds

    async def claim(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        claimed_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaim | None:
        if not isinstance(notification_key, str) or not notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")

        reference_at = claimed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if reference_at.tzinfo is None or reference_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("claimed_at must be timezone-aware.")

        claim_token = str(uuid4())
        lease_expires_at = reference_at + timedelta(seconds=self._lease_seconds)

        claimed = await self._repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=notification_key,
            claim_token=claim_token,
            claimed_at=reference_at,
            lease_expires_at=lease_expires_at,
        )

        if not claimed:
            return None

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaim(
            notification_key=notification_key,
            claim_token=claim_token,
            claimed_at=reference_at,
            lease_expires_at=lease_expires_at,
            status=RetryCandidateClaimStatus.CLAIMED,
        )

"""Contracts for controlled PI escalation retry candidate claiming."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum


class RetryCandidateClaimStatus(StrEnum):
    CLAIMED = "CLAIMED"
    NOT_CLAIMED = "NOT_CLAIMED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaim:
    notification_key: str
    claim_token: str
    claimed_at: datetime
    lease_expires_at: datetime
    status: RetryCandidateClaimStatus = RetryCandidateClaimStatus.CLAIMED

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for field_name in (
            "notification_key",
            "claim_token",
        ):
            value = getattr(self, field_name)
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(f"{field_name} must be nonblank.")

        if self.claimed_at.tzinfo is None or self.claimed_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("claimed_at must be timezone-aware.")

        if (
            self.lease_expires_at.tzinfo is None
            or self.lease_expires_at.utcoffset() is None
        ):
            raise ValueError("lease_expires_at must be timezone-aware.")

        if self.lease_expires_at <= self.claimed_at:
            raise ValueError("lease_expires_at must be after claimed_at.")

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus(StrEnum):
    NOT_ELIGIBLE = "NOT_ELIGIBLE"
    NOT_DUE = "NOT_DUE"
    RETRIED = "RETRIED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult:
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
    notification_key: str
    attempt: int
    reason: str
    dispatch_result: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult | None = None


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService:
    """Execute one policy-authorized retry without owning scheduling.

    This service:
    - retrieves the authoritative durable delivery record;
    - evaluates retry policy;
    - enforces the policy-derived due time;
    - increments FAILED attempts only through the repository;
    - preserves the attempt number for STARTED recovery;
    - delegates actual delivery to the existing dispatch service.

    It does not:
    - schedule future work;
    - sleep;
    - call an email provider directly;
    - mutate delivery state directly.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        delivery_repository: object,
        retry_policy: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
        dispatch_service: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService,
    ) -> None:
        if delivery_repository is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "delivery_repository cannot be None."
            )

        if retry_policy is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "retry_policy cannot be None."
            )

        if dispatch_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "dispatch_service cannot be None."
            )

        self._repository = delivery_repository
        self._retry_policy = retry_policy
        self._dispatch_service = dispatch_service

    async def retry(
        self,
        *,
        request: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
        email_request: object,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult:
        record = await self._repository.get_by_notification_key(
            request.notification_key
        )

        if record is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Retry requires an existing durable delivery record."
            )

        if record.notification_key != request.notification_key:
            raise ValueError(
                "Delivery record notification key does not match request."
            )

        policy_result = self._retry_policy.evaluate(
            record=record,
            reference_at=reference_at,
        )

        if (
            policy_result.decision
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
        ):
            return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
                status=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
                    .NOT_ELIGIBLE
                ),
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                reason=policy_result.reason,
            )

        if (
            policy_result.next_eligible_at is not None
            and reference_at < policy_result.next_eligible_at
        ):
            return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
                status=(
                    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
                    .NOT_DUE
                ),
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                reason=(
                    "Retry is eligible but the policy-derived "
                    "backoff window has not elapsed."
                ),
            )

        if (
            record.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        ):
            await self._repository.start_retry(
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
            )

        # STARTED recovery deliberately does not call start_retry().
        # The durable attempt remains the same because this is recovery
        # of the interrupted attempt rather than a new FAILED retry.
        dispatch_result = await self._dispatch_service.dispatch(
            request=request,
            email_request=email_request,
        )

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
                .RETRIED
            ),
            notification_key=record.notification_key,
            attempt=(
                dispatch_result.attempt
                if dispatch_result.attempt is not None
                else record.attempt
            ),
            reason=policy_result.reason,
            dispatch_result=dispatch_result,
        )

"""Contracts for controlled PI escalation retry orchestration."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus(StrEnum):
    SUCCEEDED = "SUCCEEDED"
    SKIPPED = "SKIPPED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceRetryState:
    """Authoritative state required immediately before retry dispatch."""

    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    recommendation_status: str
    review_status: str
    decision_status: str
    quality_gate_status: str
    dispatch_request: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest
    email_request: Any

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for field_name in (
            "notification_key",
            "recommendation_id",
            "recommendation_status",
            "review_status",
            "decision_status",
            "quality_gate_status",
        ):
            value = getattr(self, field_name)
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(f"{field_name} must be nonblank.")

        if self.dispatch_request.notification_key != self.notification_key:
            raise ValueError(
                "dispatch_request.notification_key must match notification_key."
            )
        if self.dispatch_request.recommendation_id != self.recommendation_id:
            raise ValueError(
                "dispatch_request.recommendation_id must match recommendation_id."
            )

class PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult:

    status: PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus
    reason: str
    attempt: int | None = None
    provider_message_id: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")
        if not self.reason.strip():
            raise ValueError("reason must be nonblank.")
        if self.attempt is not None and self.attempt < 1:
            raise ValueError("attempt must be at least 1.")

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestrator.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""Controlled orchestration for one PI escalation retry candidate."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Protocol

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claim_service import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidates import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryState,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryStateReader(Protocol):
    async def get_retry_state(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        recommendation_id: str,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryState | None: ...


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryPolicyEvaluator(Protocol):
    async def evaluate(
        self,
        *,
        candidate: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> bool: ...


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryExecutor(Protocol):
    async def retry(
        self,
        *,
        request: object,
        email_request: object,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult: ...


class PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator:
    """Coordinates policy, durable claim, state revalidation and retry.

    This service deliberately does not discover candidates, implement retry
    policy, send email directly, or mutate human-review/risk state.

    The canonical retry execution service remains the owner of retry-policy
    enforcement and existing dispatch integration. The orchestrator only
    supplies the authoritative dispatch context after the durable claim and
    final state revalidation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        state_reader: PlatformIntelligenceRetryStateReader,
        policy_evaluator: PlatformIntelligenceRetryPolicyEvaluator,
        claim_service: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService,
        retry_execution: PlatformIntelligenceRetryExecutor,
    ) -> None:
        self._state_reader = state_reader
        self._policy_evaluator = policy_evaluator
        self._claim_service = claim_service
        self._retry_execution = retry_execution

    async def run_candidate(
        self,
        *,
        candidate: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult:
        if reference_at.tzinfo is None or reference_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("reference_at must be timezone-aware.")

        eligible = await self._policy_evaluator.evaluate(
            candidate=candidate,
            reference_at=reference_at,
        )
        if not eligible:
            return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
                notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                reason="RETRY_POLICY_NOT_ELIGIBLE",
                attempt=candidate.attempt,
            )

        claim = await self._claim_service.claim(
            notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
            claimed_at=reference_at,
        )
        if claim is None:
            return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
                notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                reason="RETRY_CANDIDATE_ALREADY_CLAIMED",
                attempt=candidate.attempt,
            )

        state = await self._state_reader.get_retry_state(
            notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
            recommendation_id=candidate.recommendation_id,
        )
        if state is None:
            return self._skipped(candidate, "AUTHORITATIVE_STATE_NOT_FOUND")
        if state.notification_key != candidate.notification_key:
            return self._skipped(candidate, "NOTIFICATION_KEY_MISMATCH")
        if state.recommendation_id != candidate.recommendation_id:
            return self._skipped(candidate, "RECOMMENDATION_ID_MISMATCH")
        if state.recommendation_status != "RECOMMENDED":
            return self._skipped(
                candidate, "RECOMMENDATION_NO_LONGER_RECOMMENDED"
            )
        if state.review_status != "IN_REVIEW":
            return self._skipped(candidate, "REVIEW_NOT_IN_REVIEW")
        if state.decision_status != "PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW":
            return self._skipped(
                candidate, "DECISION_NO_LONGER_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW"
            )
        if state.quality_gate_status != "PASSED":
            return self._skipped(
                candidate, "QUALITY_GATE_NO_LONGER_PASSED"
            )

        execution = await self._retry_execution.retry(
            request=state.dispatch_request,
            email_request=state.email_request,
            reference_at=reference_at,
        )

        if (
            execution.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED
        ):
            dispatch_result = execution.dispatch_result
            return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
                notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                reason=execution.reason,
                attempt=execution.attempt,
                provider_message_id=(
                    dispatch_result.provider_message_id
                    if dispatch_result is not None
                    else None
                ),
            )

        if execution.status in (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.NOT_ELIGIBLE,
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.NOT_DUE,
        ):
            return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
                notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                reason=execution.reason,
                attempt=execution.attempt,
            )

        return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
            notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.FAILED,
            reason=execution.reason,
            attempt=execution.attempt,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _skipped(
        candidate: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
        reason: str,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult:
        return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
            notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
            reason=reason,
            attempt=candidate.attempt,
        )
