FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_retry_worker.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""Contracts for the bounded PI escalation retry worker."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus(StrEnum):
    SUCCEEDED = "SUCCEEDED"
    SKIPPED = "SKIPPED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult:
    notification_key: str
    status: PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
    reason: str
    attempt: int | None = None
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_type: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")
        if not self.reason.strip():
            raise ValueError("reason must be nonblank.")
        if self.attempt is not None and self.attempt < 1:
            raise ValueError("attempt must be at least 1.")
        if self.error_type is not None and not self.error_type.strip():
            raise ValueError("error_type must be nonblank when provided.")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult:
    requested_limit: int
    discovered_count: int
    processed_count: int
    succeeded_count: int
    skipped_count: int
    failed_count: int
    items: tuple[PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.requested_limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("requested_limit must be at least 1.")
        if self.discovered_count < 0:
            raise ValueError("discovered_count cannot be negative.")
        if self.processed_count < 0:
            raise ValueError("processed_count cannot be negative.")
        if self.processed_count != len(self.items):
            raise ValueError("processed_count must equal the number of items.")
        if self.succeeded_count < 0 or self.skipped_count < 0 or self.failed_count < 0:
            raise ValueError("result counts cannot be negative.")
        if (
            self.succeeded_count + self.skipped_count + self.failed_count
            != self.processed_count
        ):
            raise ValueError("result counts must equal processed_count.")
        if self.discovered_count > self.requested_limit:
            raise ValueError("discovered_count cannot exceed requested_limit.")
        keys = [item.notification_key for item in self.items]
        if len(keys) != len(set(keys)):
            raise ValueError("worker result cannot contain duplicate notification keys.")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest:
    limit: int = 100

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("limit must be at least 1.")


------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_retry_worker_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""Bounded, scheduler-free orchestration boundary for PI retry work."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidate_discovery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestrator import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker:
    """Process one bounded candidate batch through the governed retry chain.

    This class deliberately does not:
    - schedule itself;
    - sleep;
    - create background tasks;
    - access providers;
    - implement retry policy;
    - claim candidates directly;
    - mutate risk or human-review state.

    The caller owns invocation timing and supplies the reference timestamp.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        discovery_service: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService,
        orchestrator: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator,
    ) -> None:
        if discovery_service is None:
            raise ValueError("discovery_service cannot be None.")

        if orchestrator is None:
            raise ValueError("orchestrator cannot be None.")

        self._discovery_service = discovery_service
        self._orchestrator = orchestrator

    async def run(
        self,
        *,
        request: PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest,
        reference_at: datetime,
    ) -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult:
        if reference_at.tzinfo is None or reference_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("reference_at must be timezone-aware.")

        batch = await self._discovery_service.discover(
            limit=request.limit,
        )

        results: list[PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult] = []

        for candidate in batch.candidates:
            try:
                execution = await self._orchestrator.run_candidate(
                    candidate=candidate,
                    reference_at=reference_at,
                )

            except Exception as exc:
                results.append(
                    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
                        notification_key=candidate.notification_key,
                        status=(
                            PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
                            .FAILED
                        ),
                        reason="CANDIDATE_EXECUTION_ERROR",
                        attempt=candidate.attempt,
                        error_type=type(exc).__name__,
                    )
                )
                continue

            if (
                execution.status
                is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED
            ):
                results.append(
                    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
                        notification_key=execution.notification_key,
                        status=(
                            PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
                            .SUCCEEDED
                        ),
                        reason=execution.reason,
                        attempt=execution.attempt,
                        provider_message_id=(
                            execution.provider_message_id
                        ),
                    )
                )

            elif (
                execution.status
                is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED
            ):
                results.append(
                    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
                        notification_key=execution.notification_key,
                        status=(
                            PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
                            .SKIPPED
                        ),
                        reason=execution.reason,
                        attempt=execution.attempt,
                    )
                )

            else:
                results.append(
                    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
                        notification_key=execution.notification_key,
                        status=(
                            PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus
                            .FAILED
                        ),
                        reason=execution.reason,
                        attempt=execution.attempt,
                    )
                )

        succeeded = sum(
            item.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SUCCEEDED
            for item in results
        )

        skipped = sum(
            item.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SKIPPED
            for item in results
        )

        failed = sum(
            item.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.FAILED
            for item in results
        )

        return PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult(
            requested_limit=request.limit,
            discovered_count=len(batch.candidates),
            processed_count=len(results),
            succeeded_count=succeeded,
            skipped_count=skipped,
            failed_count=failed,
            items=tuple(results),
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\platform_intelligence_review_api.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""Application service for Platform Intelligence human-review visibility."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_source import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource,
)


class PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the canonical PI review source is not configured."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI:
    """Read-only application boundary for PI review projections."""

    source: PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource | None = None

    @property
    def configured(self) -> bool:
        return self.source is not None

    def _require_source(self) -> PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource:
        if self.source is None:
            raise PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured(
                "Platform Intelligence review source is not configured."
            )

        return self.source

    def list_reviews(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        source = self._require_source()

        items = [
            self._serialize_projection(item)
            for item in source.list_reviews()
        ]

        return {
            "status": "ready",
            "count": len(items),
            "items": items,
        }

    def get_review(self, review_item_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        if not review_item_id or not review_item_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("review_item_id must not be blank.")

        source = self._require_source()
        item = source.get_review(review_item_id)

        if item is None:
            return None

        return self._serialize_projection(item)

    def list_escalations(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        source = self._require_source()

        items = [
            self._serialize_projection(item)
            for item in source.list_escalations()
        ]

        recommended = [
            item
            for item in items
            if item.get("recommendation_status") == "RECOMMENDED"
        ]

        return {
            "status": "ready",
            "count": len(recommended),
            "items": recommended,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _serialize_projection(item: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Expose only the approved PI review projection fields."""

        def value(attribute: str) -> Any:
            result = getattr(item, attribute, None)

            if hasattr(result, "value"):
                return result.value

            return result

        created_at = getattr(item, "created_at", None)
        assigned_at = getattr(item, "assigned_at", None)
        review_due_at = getattr(item, "review_due_at", None)

        return {
            "review_item_id": value("review_item_id"),
            "risk_decision_id": value("risk_decision_id"),
            "risk_assessment_id": value("risk_assessment_id"),
            "scope": value("scope"),
            "risk_level": value("risk_level"),
            "risk_score": value("risk_score"),
            "priority": value("priority"),
            "review_status": value("review_status"),
            "decision_status": value("decision_status"),
            "quality_gate_status": value("quality_gate_status"),
            "evidence_references": tuple(
                value("evidence_references") or ()
            ),
            "historical_references": tuple(
                value("historical_references") or ()
            ),
            "policy_id": value("policy_id"),
            "policy_version": value("policy_version"),
            "created_at": (
                created_at.isoformat()
                if created_at is not None
                else None
            ),
            "assigned_reviewer": value("assigned_reviewer"),
            "assigned_at": (
                assigned_at.isoformat()
                if assigned_at is not None
                else None
            ),
            "review_due_at": (
                review_due_at.isoformat()
                if review_due_at is not None
                else None
            ),
            "rationale": value("rationale"),
            "recommendation_status": value("recommendation_status"),
            "recommendation_route": value("recommendation_route"),
            "recommendation_reasons": tuple(
                value("recommendation_reasons") or ()
            ),
            "recommendation_id": value("recommendation_id"),
        }

    def __all_projection_fields(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        """Private marker preventing accidental metadata expansion."""
        return ()


def create_platform_intelligence_review_api(
    source: PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource | None = None,
) -> PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI:
    """Create the governed PI review application boundary."""

    return PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=source)

"""Application port for the canonical Platform Intelligence review projection."""
from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from collections.abc import Sequence
from typing import Any


class PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource(ABC):
    """Read-only application port for PI human-review projections.

    The backend deliberately depends on this port rather than importing or
    constructing Platform Intelligence runtime services. A production adapter
    can bind this port to the canonical ``ai_agents`` runtime integration.
    """

    @abstractmethod
    def list_reviews(self) -> Sequence[Any]:
        """Return current governed review projections."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    def get_review(self, review_item_id: str) -> Any | None:
        """Return one governed review projection, if available."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    def list_escalations(self) -> Sequence[Any]:
        """Return governed review projections with escalation recommendations."""
        raise NotImplementedError


class UnconfiguredPlatformIntelligenceReviewSource(PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource):
    """Safe inert source object when the canonical PI integration is absent.

    The application API intentionally uses ``source=None`` to distinguish
    an unconfigured integration from a valid empty result set. This class is
    retained as an explicit port implementation for composition/tests and is
    not used as the default API source.
    """

    def list_reviews(self) -> Sequence[Any]:
        return ()

    def get_review(self, review_item_id: str) -> Any | None:
        return None

    def list_escalations(self) -> Sequence[Any]:
        return ()

"""
WS-21.17 Private Classroom Operations composition.

Creates the application-owned dependency graph for the read-only
Private Classroom operational detail API.

Architecture:

    ProductionHealthConfiguration
        -> ProductionReadinessService
        -> OperationalHealthSnapshotService
        -> OperationalEventService
        -> OperationalDashboardService
        -> PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI

This composition deliberately reuses the existing Operations Center
health and operational-event abstractions.

It does not:

- create a second incident store;
- create a second event service;
- execute agents;
- execute providers;
- modify Runtime state;
- perform remediation;
- persist operational events.

The snapshot is generated fresh for every API request.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.health.operational_dashboard_service import (
    OperationalDashboardService,
)
from app.application.health.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)
from app.application.health.operational_health_snapshot import (
    OperationalHealthSnapshotService,
)
from app.application.health.production_health_configuration import (
    ProductionHealthConfiguration,
)
from app.application.health.production_readiness_service import (
    ProductionReadinessService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.private_classroom_operations_api import (
    PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI,
)


class PrivateClassroomOperationsComposition:
    """
    Application-owned WS-21.17 dependency graph.

    The composition owns one OperationalEventService instance so the
    current in-memory operational state remains consistent across
    requests within the running application process.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        configuration = ProductionHealthConfiguration()

        readiness_service = ProductionReadinessService(
            checks=configuration.build_checks(),
        )

        health_snapshot_service = OperationalHealthSnapshotService(
            readiness_service=readiness_service,
        )

        event_service = OperationalEventService()

        dashboard_service = OperationalDashboardService(
            health_snapshot_service=health_snapshot_service,
            event_service=event_service,
        )

        self._event_service = event_service

        self._dashboard_service = dashboard_service

        self._private_classroom_api = PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI(
            dashboard_service=dashboard_service,
        )

    @property
    def event_service(self) -> OperationalEventService:
        """Return the application-owned operational event service."""
        return self._event_service

    @property
    def dashboard_service(self) -> OperationalDashboardService:
        """Return the shared Operations Center dashboard service."""
        return self._dashboard_service

    @property
    def private_classroom_api(self) -> PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI:
        """Return the WS-21.17 Private Classroom application API."""
        return self._private_classroom_api


def create_private_classroom_operations_api() -> (
    PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI
):
    """
    Create the WS-21.17 Private Classroom application boundary.
    """
    return (
        PrivateClassroomOperationsComposition()
        .private_classroom_api
    )


__all__ = [
    "PrivateClassroomOperationsComposition",
    "create_private_classroom_operations_api",
]

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass

from .privacy_approval_email_service import (
    ApprovalEmailSender,
    PrivacyApprovalEmailService,
)
from .privacy_approval_registry import PrivacyApprovalRegistry
from .privacy_change_registry import PrivacyChangeRegistry
from .privacy_governance_case_registry import (
    PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry,
)
from .privacy_governance_case_service import (
    PrivacyGovernanceCaseService,
)
from .privacy_governance_dashboard_service import (
    PrivacyGovernanceDashboardService,
)
from .privacy_governance_decision_service import (
    PrivacyGovernanceDecisionService,
)
from .privacy_governance_flow_service import (
    PrivacyGovernanceFlowService,
)
from .privacy_governance_gate import (
    PrivacyGovernanceGate,
)
from .privacy_validation_service import (
    PrivacyValidationService,
)


@dataclass(slots=True)
class PrivacyGovernanceComposition:
    """Single composition root for the privacy governance capability.

    Beta 1 uses in-memory registries.

    All application services receive the same registry instances so that:

    - case creation;
    - governance decisions;
    - approval history;
    - dashboard reads;
    - API reads

    operate on one coherent runtime state.

    Email infrastructure is optional at composition-construction time.
    This is intentional:

    - unit tests must not require external email configuration;
    - application startup must not fail merely because email is disabled;
    - actual escalation requires an explicitly configured email service.

    Repository-backed implementations can replace the registries later
    without changing the application service contracts.
    """

    change_registry: PrivacyChangeRegistry
    case_registry: PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry
    approval_registry: PrivacyApprovalRegistry

    decision_service: PrivacyGovernanceDecisionService
    governance_gate: PrivacyGovernanceGate
    case_service: PrivacyGovernanceCaseService
    flow_service: PrivacyGovernanceFlowService
    dashboard_service: PrivacyGovernanceDashboardService


def _build_runtime_approval_email_service(
    *,
    approval_email_service: PrivacyApprovalEmailService | None,
) -> PrivacyApprovalEmailService | None:
    """Resolve the optional runtime approval-email service.

    Explicit dependency injection always wins.

    When no service is injected, the service is constructed only when
    the required runtime configuration exists.

    Missing email configuration is NOT a composition-construction error.

    This is important because the privacy governance application layer
    must remain usable without an external email provider, especially in:

    - unit tests;
    - local development;
    - provider-independent deployments;
    - environments where email escalation is intentionally disabled.

    Actual governance escalation remains protected by
    PrivacyGovernanceGate.route_and_escalate(), which requires an
    approval email service before attempting delivery.
    """

    if approval_email_service is not None:
        return approval_email_service

    recipient = os.getenv(
        "PRIVACY_APPROVAL_EMAIL_TO",
        "",
    ).strip()

    if not recipient:
        return None

    try:
        from app.infrastructure.email.resend_approval_email_sender import (
            ResendApprovalEmailSender,
        )
    except ImportError:
        # Keep the application composition provider-independent.
        #
        # Infrastructure may not be installed in lightweight unit-test
        # environments.
        return None

    try:
        sender: ApprovalEmailSender = ResendApprovalEmailSender()
    except (RuntimeError, ValueError):
        # Configuration/provider availability must not prevent creation
        # of the governance application composition.
        return None

    return PrivacyApprovalEmailService(
        sender=sender,
        approval_recipient=recipient,
    )


def create_privacy_governance_composition(
    *,
    validation_service: PrivacyValidationService | None = None,
    approval_email_service: PrivacyApprovalEmailService | None = None,
) -> PrivacyGovernanceComposition:
    """Create an isolated privacy governance composition.

    This function is the primary dependency-injection boundary.

    Important design rule:

        Composition creation != external side effect.

    Creating this composition:

    - creates registries;
    - wires application services;
    - optionally wires configured infrastructure;

    but does NOT:

    - send email;
    - call Resend;
    - make governance decisions;
    - modify production systems.
    """

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Authoritative in-memory state
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    change_registry = PrivacyChangeRegistry()
    case_registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry()
    approval_registry = PrivacyApprovalRegistry()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Optional infrastructure
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    resolved_approval_email_service = (
        _build_runtime_approval_email_service(
            approval_email_service=approval_email_service,
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Decision service
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    decision_service = PrivacyGovernanceDecisionService(
        approval_registry=approval_registry,
        change_registry=change_registry,
        case_registry=case_registry,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Governance gate
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    governance_gate = PrivacyGovernanceGate(
        registry=change_registry,
        validation_service=validation_service,
        approval_email_service=resolved_approval_email_service,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Case application service
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    case_service = PrivacyGovernanceCaseService(
        case_registry=case_registry,
        change_registry=change_registry,
        approval_registry=approval_registry,
        decision_service=decision_service,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # End-to-end governance flow
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    flow_service = PrivacyGovernanceFlowService(
        change_registry=change_registry,
        governance_gate=governance_gate,
        case_service=case_service,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Dashboard/read model
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    dashboard_service = PrivacyGovernanceDashboardService(
        case_registry=case_registry,
        approval_registry=approval_registry,
    )

    return PrivacyGovernanceComposition(
        change_registry=change_registry,
        case_registry=case_registry,
        approval_registry=approval_registry,
        decision_service=decision_service,
        governance_gate=governance_gate,
        case_service=case_service,
        flow_service=flow_service,
        dashboard_service=dashboard_service,
    )


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Runtime composition
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

_runtime_composition: PrivacyGovernanceComposition | None = None


def get_privacy_governance_composition() -> PrivacyGovernanceComposition:
    """Return the process-wide Beta privacy governance composition.

    The same composition instance is reused during the application
    process so the Operations Center API and application integrations
    observe the same in-memory governance state.

    Email configuration is optional during composition creation.
    """

    global _runtime_composition

    if _runtime_composition is None:
        _runtime_composition = (
            create_privacy_governance_composition()
        )

    return _runtime_composition


def reset_privacy_governance_composition_for_tests() -> None:
    """Reset the process-wide composition.

    This function exists exclusively for test isolation.

    Production application code must not use this function to reset
    governance state.
    """

    global _runtime_composition

    _runtime_composition = None


__all__ = [
    "PrivacyGovernanceComposition",
    "create_privacy_governance_composition",
    "get_privacy_governance_composition",
    "reset_privacy_governance_composition_for_tests",
]

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\privacy\privacy_governance_decision_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from .privacy_approval_record import ApprovalDecision, PrivacyApprovalRecord
from .privacy_approval_registry import PrivacyApprovalRegistry
from .privacy_change import PrivacyChangeStatus
from .privacy_change_registry import PrivacyChangeRegistry
from .privacy_governance_case_registry import PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry


class PrivacyGovernanceDecisionService:
    """Records explicit DPO/GRC decisions and updates the governance case."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        approval_registry: PrivacyApprovalRegistry,
        change_registry: PrivacyChangeRegistry,
        case_registry: PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry,
    ) -> None:
        if approval_registry is None:
            raise ValueError("approval_registry cannot be None.")
        if change_registry is None:
            raise ValueError("change_registry cannot be None.")
        if case_registry is None:
            raise ValueError("case_registry cannot be None.")

        self._approvals = approval_registry
        self._changes = change_registry
        self._cases = case_registry

    def record_decision(
        self,
        *,
        case_id: str,
        change_id: str,
        approval_id: str,
        reviewer: str,
        role: str,
        decision: ApprovalDecision,
        comment: str,
    ) -> PrivacyApprovalRecord:
        change = self._changes.get(change_id)
        case = self._cases.get(case_id)

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # GOVERNANCE INTEGRITY CONTROL 1
        #
        # The change submitted for a decision MUST belong to the governance
        # case being decided.
        #
        # Without this check a caller could theoretically submit:
        #
        #   case_id   = CASE-A
        #   change_id = CHANGE-B
        #
        # and create approval evidence connecting unrelated governance
        # objects.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        if case.change_id != change_id:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Change {change_id} does not belong to governance case "
                f"{case_id}."
            )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # GOVERNANCE INTEGRITY CONTROL 2
        #
        # APPROVED and BLOCKED are terminal governance states.
        #
        # Normal decision endpoints must not silently reopen or overwrite a
        # finalized governance case. A future reopen workflow, if required,
        # must be explicit and separately controlled.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        if case.governance_status in {"APPROVED", "BLOCKED"}:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Governance case {case_id} already has a final decision."
            )

        record = PrivacyApprovalRecord(
            approval_id=approval_id,
            case_id=case_id,
            change_id=change_id,
            reviewer=reviewer,
            role=role.strip().upper(),
            decision=decision,
            comment=comment,
            decided_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

        self._approvals.register(record)

        records = self._approvals.for_case(case_id)

        dpo_ok = any(
            r.role == "DPO"
            and r.decision == ApprovalDecision.APPROVED
            for r in records
        )

        grc_ok = any(
            r.role == "GRC"
            and r.decision == ApprovalDecision.APPROVED
            for r in records
        )

        rejected = any(
            r.decision == ApprovalDecision.REJECTED
            for r in records
        )

        changes_requested = any(
            r.decision == ApprovalDecision.REQUEST_CHANGES
            for r in records
        )

        if rejected or changes_requested:
            status = PrivacyChangeStatus.BLOCKED
            governance_status = "BLOCKED"

        elif (
            (not change.dpo_required or dpo_ok)
            and (not change.grc_required or grc_ok)
        ):
            status = PrivacyChangeStatus.APPROVED
            governance_status = "APPROVED"

        else:
            status = PrivacyChangeStatus.PENDING_DPO_GRC
            governance_status = "PENDING_DPO_GRC"

        self._changes.update(
            replace(
                change,
                status=status,
            )
        )

        self._cases.update(
            replace(
                case,
                governance_status=governance_status,
                final_decision=(
                    decision.value
                    if governance_status in {"APPROVED", "BLOCKED"}
                    else None
                ),
                final_decision_at=(
                    record.decided_at
                    if governance_status in {"APPROVED", "BLOCKED"}
                    else None
                ),
            )
        )

        return record

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\privacy\privacy_governance_reminder_scheduler.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Protocol

from .privacy_governance_reminder_history import (
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory,
)
from .privacy_governance_reminder_policy import (
    GovernanceReminderPolicy,
    build_decision_proposal,
)
from .privacy_governance_reminder_result import (
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult,
)


class GovernanceReminderStatePort(Protocol):
    def get_last_sent(self, case_id: str) -> Any:
        ...

    def record(
        self,
        *,
        case_id: str,
        sent_at: datetime,
        recipient_role: str,
        email_reference: str | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        ...


class GovernanceReminderEmailPort(Protocol):
    def send_reminder(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        proposal: Any,
    ) -> None:
        ...


class PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort(Protocol):
    async def send_governance_reminder(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        recipient_role: str,
        reason: str,
    ) -> Any:
        ...


class InMemoryGovernanceReminderState(
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory
):
    """Process-local Beta reminder state."""

    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class GovernanceReminderRunResult:
    """Aggregate result for the operational follow-up invocation."""

    scanned: int
    reminded: int
    case_ids_reminded: tuple[str, ...]


class PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistryPort(Protocol):
    def all(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
        ...

    def get(self, case_id: str) -> Any:
        ...


class PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler:
    """Single governance reminder workflow with compatibility contracts.

    Supported application contract:

        run_once()
        reminder_state
        email_sender

    Supported cybersecurity contract:

        run()
        run_case()
        history
        email_service

    This class is not an APScheduler implementation. It is an application
    workflow invoked by the approved scheduler runtime.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        case_registry: Any,
        policy: GovernanceReminderPolicy,
        reminder_state: GovernanceReminderStatePort | None = None,
        email_sender: GovernanceReminderEmailPort | None = None,
        history: PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory | None = None,
        email_service: PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if case_registry is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "case_registry cannot be None."
            )

        if policy is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "policy cannot be None."
            )

        self.case_registry = case_registry
        self.policy = policy

        self.reminder_state = (
            reminder_state
            or history
            or InMemoryGovernanceReminderState()
        )

        self.email_sender = email_sender
        self.email_service = email_service

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 6J operational follow-up API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def run_once(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> GovernanceReminderRunResult:
        """Run the synchronous operational follow-up contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        cases = tuple(
            self.case_registry.all()
        )

        reminded: list[str] = []

        for case in cases:
            if self._run_sync_case(
                case,
                now=current,
            ):
                reminded.append(
                    case.case_id
                )

        return GovernanceReminderRunResult(
            scanned=len(cases),
            reminded=len(reminded),
            case_ids_reminded=tuple(reminded),
        )

    def _run_sync_case(
        self,
        case: Any,
        *,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> bool:
        if not self.policy.is_due(
            case,
            history=self.reminder_state,
            now=now,
        ):
            return False

        if self.email_sender is None:
            return False

        proposal = build_decision_proposal(case)

        self.email_sender.send_reminder(
            case=case,
            proposal=proposal,
        )

        self.reminder_state.record(
            case_id=case.case_id,
            sent_at=now,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
        )

        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Existing cybersecurity async API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    async def run(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult, ...]:
        """Evaluate all cases through the async email-service contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        results: list[
            PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult
        ] = []

        for case in self.case_registry.all():
            results.append(
                await self._evaluate_case(
                    case,
                    now=current,
                )
            )

        return tuple(results)

    async def run_case(
        self,
        case_id: str,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult:
        """Evaluate one case through the async contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        case = self.case_registry.get(
            case_id
        )

        return await self._evaluate_case(
            case,
            now=current,
        )

    async def _evaluate_case(
        self,
        case: Any,
        *,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult:
        if not self.policy.is_eligible(case):
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="NOT_ELIGIBLE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=False,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason="case_not_pending_dpo_grc",
                generated_at=now,
            )

        if not self.policy.is_due(
            case,
            history=self.reminder_state,
            now=now,
        ):
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="NOT_DUE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=False,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason="reminder_interval_or_cooldown_not_reached",
                generated_at=now,
            )

        reason = (
            "pending_dpo_grc_reminder_due"
        )

        if self.email_service is None:
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="DUE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=True,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason=reason,
                generated_at=now,
                email_status="not_configured",
            )

        try:
            response = (
                await self.email_service.send_governance_reminder(
                    case=case,
                    recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                    reason=reason,
                )
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="EMAIL_FAILED",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=True,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason=reason,
                generated_at=now,
                email_status="failed",
                error=str(exc),
            )

        email_status = "sent"
        reference: str | None = None
        case_url: str | None = None

        if isinstance(response, dict):
            email_status = str(
                response.get(
                    "status",
                    "sent",
                )
            )

            value = (
                response.get("reference")
                or response.get("message_id")
            )

            if value is not None:
                reference = str(value)

            url = response.get("case_url")

            if url is not None:
                case_url = str(url)

        elif response is not None:
            reference = str(response)

        self.reminder_state.record(
            case_id=case.case_id,
            sent_at=now,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
            email_reference=reference,
        )

        return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
            status="REMINDER_SENT",
            case_id=case.case_id,
            governance_status=case.governance_status,
            due=True,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
            reason=reason,
            generated_at=now,
            email_status=email_status,
            email_reference=reference,
            case_url=case_url,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_now(
        now: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        if now.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "now must be timezone-aware."
            )


__all__ = [
    "GovernanceReminderEmailPort",
    "GovernanceReminderRunResult",
    "GovernanceReminderStatePort",
    "InMemoryGovernanceReminderState",
    "PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistryPort",
    "PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort",
    "PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler",
]

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.provider_management.functionality_control_authorizer import (
    AuthorizationFunctionalityControlAuthorizer,
)
from app.application.provider_management.functionality_operational_control_service import (
    FunctionalityOperationalControlService,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_functionality_control_repository import (
    PostgreSQLFunctionalityControlRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_database_engine,
    create_session_factory,
)


def create_functionality_control_service(
    *,
    session_factory,
) -> FunctionalityOperationalControlService:
    """Create the approved functionality-control application service.

    The caller owns the database engine/session lifecycle.
    This helper only composes the application service boundary.
    """

    repository = PostgreSQLFunctionalityControlRepository(
        session_factory,
    )

    authorizer = AuthorizationFunctionalityControlAuthorizer()

    return FunctionalityOperationalControlService(
        authorizer=authorizer,
        repository=repository,
    )


def create_standalone_functionality_control_service() -> tuple[
    FunctionalityOperationalControlService,
    object,
]:
    """Create a control service for a standalone runtime process.

    Returns both the service and its synchronous database engine so
    the caller can dispose the engine during shutdown.
    """

    engine = create_database_engine()
    session_factory = create_session_factory(engine)

    service = create_functionality_control_service(
        session_factory=session_factory,
    )

    return service, engine


__all__ = [
    "create_functionality_control_service",
    "create_standalone_functionality_control_service",
]

"""EasyChatBot AI Operations Center composition root.

The same ProviderHealthHistoryRepository instance is shared by:
- provider-health observation ingestion;
- provider intelligence analytics.

This keeps dashboard intelligence aligned with the observed
provider-health lifecycle.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass

    InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository,
    ProviderIncidentService,
)

    ProviderOperationalEventService,
)
from providers.health.ollama_cloud_health_probe import (
    OllamaCloudHealthProbe,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OperationsCenterComposition:
    """Composed Operations Center application graph."""

    dashboard_api: OperationsCenterDashboardAPI
    health_application_service: ProviderHealthApplicationService
    operational_event_service: OperationalEventService
    operational_events_api: OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI
    health_refresh_service: ProviderHealthRefreshService | None


def create_operations_center_composition() -> OperationsCenterComposition:
    """Construct the complete Operations Center application graph."""

    health_monitor = ProviderHealthMonitorService()

    # One repository is intentionally shared across health ingestion
    # and provider intelligence.
    provider_health_history_repository = ProviderHealthHistoryRepository()

    provider_intelligence_service = (
        OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceService(
            repository=provider_health_history_repository,
        )
    )

    operational_event_repository = InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()

    operational_event_service = OperationalEventService(
        repository=operational_event_repository,
    )

    provider_operational_event_service = ProviderOperationalEventService(
        operational_event_service=operational_event_service,
    )

    operational_events_api = OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI(
        operational_event_service=operational_event_service,
    )

    incident_repository = InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository()

    incident_service = ProviderIncidentService(
        repository=incident_repository,
        operational_event_service=provider_operational_event_service,
    )

    # IMPORTANT: use the SAME history repository instance here.
    health_application_service = ProviderHealthApplicationService(
        health_monitor=health_monitor,
        incident_service=incident_service,
        history_repository=provider_health_history_repository,
    )

    health_refresh_service = None

    if os.getenv("OLLAMA_API_KEY"):
        ollama_probe = OllamaCloudHealthProbe()

        probe_runner = ProviderHealthProbeRunner(
            health_application_service=health_application_service,
            probes=[ollama_probe],
        )

        health_refresh_service = ProviderHealthRefreshService(
            probe_runner=probe_runner,
        )

    workflow_repository = InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository()

    history_service = EscalationHistoryService(
        repository=workflow_repository,
    )

    provider_service = OperationsCenterProviderService(
        health_monitor=health_monitor,
    )

    dashboard_service = OperationsCenterDashboardService(
        history_service=history_service,
        provider_service=provider_service,
        provider_intelligence_service=provider_intelligence_service,
    )

    facade = OperationsCenterDashboardFacade(
        dashboard_service=dashboard_service,
    )

    dashboard_api = OperationsCenterDashboardAPI(
        facade=facade,
    )

    return OperationsCenterComposition(
        dashboard_api=dashboard_api,
        health_application_service=health_application_service,
        operational_event_service=operational_event_service,
        operational_events_api=operational_events_api,
        health_refresh_service=health_refresh_service,
    )


def create_operations_center_dashboard_api() -> OperationsCenterDashboardAPI:
    """Backward-compatible dashboard API composition entry point."""
    return create_operations_center_composition().dashboard_api


__all__ = [
    "OperationsCenterComposition",
    "create_operations_center_composition",
    "create_operations_center_dashboard_api",
]

------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot provider health composition.

Builds the provider-health application graph used by the
Operations Center.

Composition only:

    OllamaCloudHealthProbe
            |
            v
    ProviderHealthProbeRunner
            |
            v
    ProviderHealthApplicationService
            |
            +-----------------------------+
            |                             |
            v                             v
    ProviderHealthMonitorService   ProviderIncidentService
            |                             |
            v                             v
    ProviderHealthReadModel        Incident lifecycle

This module does NOT:

- implement provider-specific health logic;
- call provider APIs directly;
- calculate provider health;
- render dashboard data;
- modify Runtime routing;
- perform autonomous failover;
- perform autonomous remediation.

Provider-specific probes are injected into the generic
ProviderHealthProbeRunner.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_application_service import (
    ProviderHealthApplicationService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_monitor_service import (
    ProviderHealthMonitorService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_probe_runner import (
    ProviderHealthProbeRunner,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_incident_service import (
    InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository,
    ProviderIncidentService,
)
from providers.health.ollama_cloud_health_probe import (
    OllamaCloudHealthProbe,
)


DEFAULT_OLLAMA_CLOUD_MODEL = "gpt-oss:120b"


def create_provider_health_probe_runner(
    *,
    ollama_api_key: str | None = None,
    ollama_model: str | None = None,
    ollama_timeout_seconds: float = 10.0,
    health_monitor: ProviderHealthMonitorService | None = None,
    incident_service: ProviderIncidentService | None = None,
) -> ProviderHealthProbeRunner:
    """
    Build the provider-health runner for the Operations Center.

    Configuration is supplied explicitly where possible.
    Environment variables are used only at this composition
    boundary for the provider credential and model selection.

    The provider API key never leaves this backend composition
    boundary and is never exposed to the frontend.
    """

    api_key = (
        ollama_api_key
        if ollama_api_key is not None
        else os.getenv("OLLAMA_API_KEY", "")
    )

    model = (
        ollama_model
        if ollama_model is not None
        else os.getenv(
            "OLLAMA_CLOUD_MODEL",
            DEFAULT_OLLAMA_CLOUD_MODEL,
        )
    )

    if not api_key.strip():
        raise ValueError(
            "OLLAMA_API_KEY is required to configure "
            "the Ollama Cloud provider health probe."
        )

    if not model.strip():
        raise ValueError(
            "Ollama Cloud model cannot be empty."
        )

    if ollama_timeout_seconds <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            "Ollama Cloud timeout must be positive."
        )

    monitor = (
        health_monitor
        if health_monitor is not None
        else ProviderHealthMonitorService()
    )

    incidents = (
        incident_service
        if incident_service is not None
        else ProviderIncidentService(
            repository=InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository(),
        )
    )

    application_service = ProviderHealthApplicationService(
        health_monitor=monitor,
        incident_service=incidents,
    )

    ollama_probe = OllamaCloudHealthProbe(
        api_key=api_key,
        model=model,
        timeout_seconds=ollama_timeout_seconds,
    )

    return ProviderHealthProbeRunner(
        health_application_service=application_service,
        probes=(ollama_probe,),
    )


__all__ = [
    "DEFAULT_OLLAMA_CLOUD_MODEL",
    "create_provider_health_probe_runner",
]

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\domain\learning_assessment\reinforcement_decision.py

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum

class ReinforcementPriority(str, Enum):
    NONE = "NONE"
    MEDIUM = "MEDIUM"
    HIGH = "HIGH"

@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ReinforcementDecision:
    skill_id: str
    priority: ReinforcementPriority
    reason: str
    recommended_repetition: bool

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.skill_id, str) or not self.skill_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("skill_id must be a non-empty string.")
        if not isinstance(self.priority, ReinforcementPriority):
            raise ValueError("priority must be a ReinforcementPriority.")
        if not isinstance(self.reason, str) or not self.reason.strip():
            raise ValueError("reason must be a non-empty string.")
        if not isinstance(self.recommended_repetition, bool):
            raise ValueError("recommended_repetition must be a boolean.")

def _required_environment(name: str) -> str:
    value = os.getenv(name, "").strip()

    if not value:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{name} is required for authentication email composition."
        )

    return value


def _create_announcement_components():
    telegram_client = TelegramAnnouncementClient()

    announcement_publisher = TelegramAnnouncementPublisher(
        telegram_client=telegram_client,
    )

    return (
        telegram_client,
        announcement_publisher,
    )


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(
    application: FastAPI,
):
    scheduler_runtime = application.state.scheduler_runtime

    try:
        await scheduler_runtime.start()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler startup failed:",
            exc,
        )

    yield

    try:
        await scheduler_runtime.stop()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler shutdown failed:",
            exc,
        )

    finally:
        telegram_client = getattr(
            application.state,
            "announcement_telegram_client",
            None,
        )

        if telegram_client is not None:
            try:
                await telegram_client.close()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "Telegram announcement client shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )

        auth_engine = getattr(
            application.state,
            "auth_engine",
            None,
        )

        if auth_engine is not None:
            try:
                await auth_engine.dispose()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "PostgreSQL authentication engine shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )

        provider_usage_engine = getattr(
            application.state,
            "provider_usage_engine",
            None,
        )

        if provider_usage_engine is not None:
            try:
                provider_usage_engine.dispose()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "PostgreSQL provider-usage engine shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )


def create_application() -> FastAPI:
    academy_config = (
        AcademyActivationConfig.from_environment()
    )

    auth_engine = create_async_database_engine()

    auth_session_factory = (
        create_async_session_factory(
            auth_engine,
        )
    )

    auth_repository = (
        PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository(
            auth_session_factory,
        )
    )

    email_provider_manager = EmailProviderManager()

    authentication_email_sender = AuthenticationEmailSenderAdapter(
        provider_manager=email_provider_manager,
    )

    authentication_email_service = AuthenticationEmailService(
        authentication_email_sender,
        frontend_base_url=_required_environment(
            "EASYCHATBOT_PORTAL_URL",
        ),
    )

    authentication_service = AuthenticationService(
        auth_repository,
        email_service=authentication_email_service,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Developer Administration
    #
    # Production path:
    #
    #   DeveloperAdminService
    #          |
    #          +--> DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory
    #                  |
    #                  +--> fresh PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWork
    #                          |
    #                          +--> fresh AsyncSession
    #                          |
    #                          +--> fresh transaction
    #                          |
    #                          +--> auth_user_roles
    #                          |
    #                          +--> developer_admin_audit_events
    #          |
    #          +--> DeveloperAdminAuditService
    #
    # Each Developer Admin mutation receives a fresh Unit-of-Work
    # instance. The role mutation and its security audit evidence
    # therefore remain within the same database transaction while
    # mutable UoW/session state is never shared between operations.
    #
    # The standalone audit repository owns transactions only when its
    # standalone ``record`` operation is used. The production Developer
    # Admin mutation uses ``record_in_session`` through the UoW.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    developer_admin_unit_of_work_factory = (
        PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory(
            auth_session_factory,
        )
    )

    developer_admin_audit_service = (
        DeveloperAdminAuditService()
    )

    developer_admin_service = DeveloperAdminService(
        repository=auth_repository,
        unit_of_work_factory=developer_admin_unit_of_work_factory,
        audit_service=developer_admin_audit_service,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # W15.2.1 Transaction Authorization
    #
    # Transaction authorization is composed once at the application root.
    # The registry and consumer are application-lifetime components and
    # provide their own synchronization boundaries.
    #
    # Authentication assurance remains a separate trusted-evidence
    # boundary. This composition does not manufacture MFA/A2 evidence.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    authorization_service = AuthorizationService()

    transaction_authorization_registry = (
        TransactionAuthorizationRegistry()
    )

    transaction_authorization_consumer = (
        TransactionAuthorizationConsumer(
            transaction_authorization_registry,
        )
    )

    transaction_authorization_service = (
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationService(
            authorization_service=authorization_service,
            registry=transaction_authorization_registry,
            consumer=transaction_authorization_consumer,
        )
    )

    trusted_clock = SystemTrustedClock()

    developer_admin_transaction_service = (
        DeveloperAdminTransactionService(
            developer_admin_service=developer_admin_service,
            transaction_authorization_service=(
                transaction_authorization_service
            ),
            clock=trusted_clock,
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Provider usage / synchronous application persistence
    #
    # The provider-usage repository is synchronous by contract.
    # It therefore receives a synchronous engine/session factory.
    #
    # It must not receive the async authentication session factory.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    provider_usage_engine = (
        create_database_engine()
    )

    persistence_diagnostics_service = PersistenceDiagnosticsService(
        metadata=Base.metadata,

    user_policy_profile_repository = (

    app_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

"""
Project Control Center production composition.

This module assembles the Project Control Center application service
from authoritative application-layer dependencies.

Responsibilities:

- construct the approved PCD source adapters;
- inject authoritative application services/registries;
- construct ProjectControlService.

This module does NOT:

- own authoritative project state;
- duplicate Health state;
- duplicate Operations state;
- duplicate Privacy Governance state;
- call providers directly;
- mutate production systems;
- execute project-management decisions.

Architecture:

    Authoritative Application Services / Registries
                         |
                         v
                 PCD Source Adapters
                         |
                         v
                ProjectControlService
                         |
                         v
                ProjectControlSnapshot
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.health.operational_dashboard_service import (
    OperationalDashboardService,
)
from app.application.health.production_health_service import (
    ProductionHealthService,
)
from app.application.privacy.privacy_governance_case_registry import (
    PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry,
)

from ..application.service import ProjectControlService
from ..domain.ports import ProjectRegistry, SnapshotRepository
from .governance_risk_source import (
    GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource,
)
from .health_source import (
    ProductionHealthProjectStatusSource,
)
from .operations_source import (
    OperationsProjectStatusSource,
)


def create_project_control_service(
    *,
    registry: ProjectRegistry,
    snapshots: SnapshotRepository,
    health_service: ProductionHealthService,
    dashboard_service: OperationalDashboardService,
    case_registry: PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry,
) -> ProjectControlService:
    """
    Create the production ProjectControlService.

    All dependencies are supplied explicitly.

    The function constructs only the PCD read-only adapters. The
    authoritative application services and registries remain owned
    by their respective bounded contexts.

    Args:
        registry:
            Authoritative PCD project registry.

        snapshots:
            PCD snapshot repository.

        health_service:
            Existing ProductionHealthService.

        dashboard_service:
            Existing OperationalDashboardService.

        case_registry:
            Existing PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry.

    Returns:
        Fully composed ProjectControlService.

    Raises:
        ValueError:
            If a required dependency is None.
    """

    if registry is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "registry cannot be None."
        )

    if snapshots is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "snapshots cannot be None."
        )

    if health_service is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "health_service cannot be None."
        )

    if dashboard_service is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "dashboard_service cannot be None."
        )

    if case_registry is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "case_registry cannot be None."
        )

    health_source = ProductionHealthProjectStatusSource(
        health_service=health_service,
    )

    operations_source = OperationsProjectStatusSource(
        dashboard_service=dashboard_service,
    )

    governance_source = GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
        case_registry=case_registry,
    )

    return ProjectControlService(
        registry=registry,
        sources=(
            health_source,
            operations_source,
            governance_source,
        ),
        snapshots=snapshots,
    )


__all__ = [
    "create_project_control_service",
]

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\project_control_center\infrastructure\governance_risk_source.py

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from app.application.privacy.privacy_governance_case_registry import (
    PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry,
)

from ..domain.models import (
    ActionSnapshot,
    IssueSnapshot,
    MetricSnapshot,
    MilestoneSnapshot,
    RiskSnapshot,
    Severity,
    SourceSnapshot,
    SourceStatus,
    WorkstreamSnapshot,
)
from ..domain.ports import ProjectStatusSource


class GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(ProjectStatusSource):
    """Read-only PCD adapter over Privacy Governance risk state.

    The PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry remains authoritative.

    This adapter projects governance-case risk into the PCD RiskSnapshot
    contract without creating a second risk store or changing governance
    state.

    Risk severity and governance decision status remain separate concepts:

        case.risk              -> RiskSnapshot.severity
        case.governance_status -> RiskSnapshot.status
    """

    _SOURCE_ID = "privacy-governance"
    _SOURCE_NAME = "Privacy Governance"

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        case_registry: PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry,
    ) -> None:
        if case_registry is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "case_registry cannot be None."
            )

        self._case_registry = case_registry

    @property
    def source_id(self) -> str:
        return self._SOURCE_ID

    @property
    def source_name(self) -> str:
        return self._SOURCE_NAME

    def health(self) -> SourceSnapshot:
        """Report adapter availability without modifying governance state."""
        return SourceSnapshot(
            source_id=self.source_id,
            name=self.source_name,
            status=SourceStatus.AVAILABLE,
            updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

    def fetch_workstreams(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[WorkstreamSnapshot]:
        """Privacy Governance does not own PCD project workstreams."""
        return []

    def fetch_metrics(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MetricSnapshot]:
        """Privacy Governance risk cases are projected as risks, not metrics."""
        return []

    def fetch_issues(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[IssueSnapshot]:
        """Privacy Governance cases are represented through RiskSnapshot."""
        return []

    def fetch_risks(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[RiskSnapshot]:
        """Project authoritative governance cases into PCD risks."""
        risks: list[RiskSnapshot] = []

        for case in self._case_registry.all():
            risks.append(
                RiskSnapshot(
                    risk_id=case.case_id,
                    project_id=project_id,
                    title=case.title,
                    severity=self._map_severity(case.risk),
                    status=case.governance_status,
                    workstream_id=None,
                    owner=None,
                    source=self.source_id,
                )
            )

        return risks

    def fetch_actions(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[ActionSnapshot]:
        """Privacy Governance does not own PCD project actions."""
        return []

    def fetch_milestones(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MilestoneSnapshot]:
        """Privacy Governance does not own PCD milestones."""
        return []

    @staticmethod
    def _map_severity(
        risk: str,
    ) -> Severity:
        """Normalize authoritative privacy risk into PCD severity.

        Privacy governance currently uses LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH semantics.
        Unknown values fail closed to MEDIUM rather than inventing a new
        severity or silently treating the case as LOW.
        """
        normalized = str(risk).strip().lower()

        mapping = {
            "low": Severity.LOW,
            "medium": Severity.MEDIUM,
            "high": Severity.HIGH,
        }

        return mapping.get(
            normalized,
            Severity.MEDIUM,
        )


__all__ = [
    "GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource",
]

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def test_application_composes_academy_into_existing_scheduler():
    """
    Verify application-level composition injects Academy into the
    existing shared scheduler boundary.

    No real scheduler is started.
    No email or Telegram delivery occurs.
    """

    fake_scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )
    fake_scheduler.running = False

    fake_academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    fake_scheduler_runtime = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_runtime",
    )

    fake_scheduler_runtime.scheduler_adapter = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_adapter",
    )

    fake_scheduler_runtime.scheduler_adapter.scheduler = (
        fake_scheduler
    )

    fake_scheduler_runtime.scheduler_adapter.academy_one_week_workflow = (
        fake_academy_workflow
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=fake_scheduler,
    ), patch(
        "backend.app.application.composition.academy_production.create_academy_one_week_workflow",
        return_value=fake_academy_workflow,
    ), patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.create_academy_one_week_workflow",
        return_value=fake_academy_workflow,
    ), patch(
        "backend.app.main.create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime",
        return_value=fake_scheduler_runtime,
    ) as create_runtime:

        import app.main as main

        assert main is not None

        create_runtime.assert_not_called()


def test_application_module_does_not_start_scheduler_during_import():
    """
    Importing the application module must not start APScheduler.
    """

    fake_scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    fake_scheduler.running = False

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=fake_scheduler,
    ):

        import app.main as main

        assert main is not None

    assert fake_scheduler.start.called is False
    assert fake_scheduler.running is False


def test_application_scheduler_boundary_remains_singleton():
    """
    The application must continue using the existing scheduler
    runtime boundary rather than creating an Academy scheduler.
    """

    fake_scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    fake_scheduler.running = False

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=fake_scheduler,
    ):

        from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory import (
            create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime,
        )

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=MagicMock(
                name="cybersecurity_workflow",
            ),
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    assert adapter.scheduler is fake_scheduler

    assert (
        adapter.academy_one_week_workflow
        is academy_workflow
    )

    assert fake_scheduler.start.called is False
    assert fake_scheduler.running is False

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from app.infrastructure.telegram.telegram_announcement_client import (
    TelegramAnnouncementClient,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_academy_activation_through_fastapi_lifespan():
    """
    Controlled FastAPI lifespan activation smoke test.

    Verifies the complete application lifecycle:

        FastAPI lifespan
            ↓
        existing shared scheduler
            ↓
        Academy registration
            ↓
        scheduler running
            ↓
        clean shutdown

    No real email or Telegram delivery is performed.
    """

    fake_engine = MagicMock(
        name="auth_engine",
    )

    fake_session_factory = MagicMock(
        name="auth_session_factory",
    )

    fake_telegram_client = MagicMock(
        spec=TelegramAnnouncementClient,
        name="announcement_telegram_client",
    )

    fake_announcement_publisher = MagicMock(
        name="announcement_publisher",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow.run = MagicMock(
        name="academy_workflow_run",
    )

    scheduler_runtime = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_runtime",
    )

    scheduler_runtime.start = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_runtime_start",
    )

    scheduler_runtime.stop = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_runtime_stop",
    )

    with (
        patch.dict(
            "os.environ",
            {
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_SCHEDULER_ENABLED": "true",
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_FIRST_RUN_ENABLED": "true",
            },
            clear=False,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_database_engine",
            return_value=fake_engine,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_session_factory",
            return_value=fake_session_factory,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository",
            return_value=MagicMock(
                name="auth_repository",
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main._create_announcement_components",
            return_value=(
                fake_telegram_client,
                fake_announcement_publisher,
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_academy_one_week_workflow",
            return_value=academy_workflow,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime",
            return_value=scheduler_runtime,
        ) as scheduler_factory,
    ):
        from app.main import create_application

        application = create_application()

        assert (
            application.state.scheduler_runtime
            is scheduler_runtime
        )

        scheduler_factory.assert_called_once_with(
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

        async with application.router.lifespan_context(
            application,
        ):
            scheduler_runtime.start.assert_called_once()

            scheduler_runtime.stop.assert_not_called()

            academy_workflow.run.assert_not_called()

            fake_telegram_client.method_calls == []

        scheduler_runtime.stop.assert_called_once()

    fake_telegram_client.method_calls == []

def _create_dependencies():
    session_factory = MagicMock(
        name="session_factory",
    )

    provider_manager = MagicMock(
        spec=EmailProviderManager,
        name="provider_manager",
    )

    return (
        session_factory,
        provider_manager,
    )


def test_academy_composition_builds_with_injected_dependencies():
    """

    with explicitly injected infrastructure dependencies.
    """

        session_factory,
        provider_manager,
    ) = _create_dependencies()

    workflow = create_academy_one_week_workflow(
        session_factory=session_factory,
        email_provider_manager=provider_manager,
        portal_url="https://example.com",
        registration_email="academy@example.com",

    assert isinstance(
        workflow,
        AcademyOneWeekWorkflow,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        workflow._users,
        PostgreSQLAcademyUserQuery,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        workflow._deliveries,
        PostgreSQLAcademyDeliveryRepository,
    )

    assert workflow._users._session_factory is session_factory
    assert workflow._deliveries._session_factory is session_factory

        ._provider_manager
        is provider_manager
    )

        session_factory,

def test_existing_scheduler_accepts_academy_workflow():
    """
    Academy must be injected into the existing shared scheduler.
    """

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

        workflow_service=MagicMock(),

    assert (
        runtime
        .scheduler_adapter
        .academy_one_week_workflow
        is academy_workflow
    )


def test_existing_scheduler_is_not_started_during_composition():
    """
    Creating the scheduler runtime must not start APScheduler.
    """

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
        workflow_service=MagicMock(),
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    assert (
        runtime
        .scheduler_adapter
        .scheduler
        .running
        is False
    )


def test_academy_scheduler_composition_creates_only_one_scheduler():
    """
    The existing scheduler owns the scheduler instance.

    Academy receives the workflow but does not own scheduler
    infrastructure.
    """

    academy_workflow = create_academy_one_week_workflow(
        session_factory=MagicMock(
            name="session_factory",
        ),
        email_provider_manager=MagicMock(
            spec=EmailProviderManager,
            name="provider_manager",
        ),
        portal_url="https://example.com",
        registration_email="academy@example.com",
    )

    runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
        workflow_service=MagicMock(),
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    assert adapter.scheduler is not None

    assert adapter.scheduler.running is False

    assert (
        adapter.academy_one_week_workflow
        is academy_workflow
    )

    assert isinstance(
        academy_workflow,
        AcademyOneWeekWorkflow,
    )

    # Academy workflow does not own scheduler infrastructure.
    assert "scheduler" not in vars(academy_workflow)
