    monkeypatch,

            email_provider_manager=MagicMock(
                spec=EmailProviderManager,

            registration_email="academy@example.com",
        )

    """

    )

    with pytest.raises(
        RuntimeError,
        match="EASYCHATBOT_REGISTRATION_EMAIL",

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from app.infrastructure.telegram.telegram_announcement_client import (
    TelegramAnnouncementClient,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_academy_real_shared_scheduler_lifespan_smoke():
    """
    Controlled end-to-end scheduler lifecycle smoke test.

    Uses:
    - real FastAPI lifespan;
    - real CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime;
    - real CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter;
    - real APScheduler.

    Academy execution and external delivery remain mocked.

    No real email or Telegram delivery occurs.
    """

    fake_engine = MagicMock(
        name="auth_engine",
    )

    fake_session_factory = MagicMock(
        name="auth_session_factory",
    )

    telegram_client = MagicMock(
        spec=TelegramAnnouncementClient,
        name="announcement_telegram_client",
    )

    announcement_publisher = MagicMock(
        name="announcement_publisher",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_execution_count = 0

    async def academy_run():
        nonlocal academy_execution_count

        academy_execution_count += 1

        return {
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        }

    academy_workflow.run = academy_run

    with (
        patch.dict(
            "os.environ",
            {
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_SCHEDULER_ENABLED": "true",
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_FIRST_RUN_ENABLED": "true",
            },
            clear=False,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_database_engine",
            return_value=fake_engine,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_session_factory",
            return_value=fake_session_factory,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository",
            return_value=MagicMock(
                name="auth_repository",
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main._create_announcement_components",
            return_value=(
                telegram_client,
                announcement_publisher,
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_academy_one_week_workflow",
            return_value=academy_workflow,
        ),
    ):
        from app.main import create_application

        application = create_application()

        runtime = application.state.scheduler_runtime
        adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter
        scheduler = adapter.scheduler

        #
        # Application construction must not start scheduling.
        #

        assert scheduler.running is False

        async with application.router.lifespan_context(
            application,
        ):
            #
            # FastAPI lifespan starts the existing scheduler.
            #

            assert scheduler.running is True

            jobs = scheduler.get_jobs()

            job_ids = {
                job.id
                for job in jobs
            }

            #
            # Existing cybersecurity jobs remain registered.
            #

            assert (
                "cybersecurity_daily_report"
                in job_ids
            )

            assert (
                "cybersecurity_weekly_report"
                in job_ids
            )

            #
            # Academy is registered on the same scheduler.
            #

            assert (
                "academy_one_week_milestone"
                in job_ids
            )

            assert (
                adapter.academy_one_week_workflow
                is academy_workflow
            )

            #
            # Starting the scheduler must not execute the Academy
            # cron job immediately.
            #

            await asyncio.sleep(0.05)

            assert academy_execution_count == 0

            #
            # No Telegram delivery may occur.
            #
            # close() is intentionally excluded because it is normal
            # FastAPI lifespan cleanup and is not a delivery operation.
            #

            telegram_delivery_calls = [
                call
                for call in telegram_client.method_calls
                if call[0] not in {"close"}
            ]

            assert telegram_delivery_calls == []

        #
        # FastAPI lifespan has exited and requested scheduler shutdown.
        #

        for _ in range(20):
            if scheduler.running is False:
                break

            await asyncio.sleep(0.01)

        assert scheduler.running is False

    #
    # Academy must never have executed during this smoke test.
    #

    assert academy_execution_count == 0

    #
    # Telegram close is expected lifecycle cleanup.
    #

    telegram_client.close.assert_called_once()

    #
    # No Telegram delivery operation occurred.
    #

    telegram_delivery_calls = [
        call
        for call in telegram_client.method_calls
        if call[0] not in {"close"}
    ]

    assert telegram_delivery_calls == []

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\academy\test_academy_real_postgresql_retry.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.academy.academy_one_week_workflow import (
    AcademyOneWeekWorkflow,
)
from app.infrastructure.academy.postgresql_academy_notification_repository import (
    PostgreSQLAcademyDeliveryRepository,
    PostgreSQLAcademyUserQuery,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


class ControlledNotifications:
    """Fail once, then succeed."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0

    @staticmethod
    def is_one_week_eligible(
        *,
        registered_at: datetime,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> bool:
        age = now - registered_at

        return (
            timedelta(days=7)
            <= age
            < timedelta(days=8)
        )

    async def send_one_week(
        self,
        *,
        email: str,
        username: str,
        telegram_chat_id: str | None,
    ) -> dict:
        self.calls += 1

        if self.calls == 1:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "controlled integration failure"
            )

        return {
            "notification_type": "1_WEEK_MILESTONE",
            "email": {
                "status": "mocked",
                "message_id": "retry-integration-message-id",
            },
            "telegram": None,
        }


async def _create_test_user(
    session_factory,
    *,
    user_id,
    email: str,
    username: str,
    created_at: datetime,
) -> None:
    async with session_factory() as session:
        await session.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO users (
                    id,
                    email,
                    username,
                    status,
                    trial_status,
                    created_at
                )
                VALUES (
                    :id,
                    :email,
                    :username,
                    'active',
                    'active',
                    :created_at
                )
                """
            ),
            {
                "id": user_id,
                "email": email,
                "username": username,
                "created_at": created_at,
            },
        )

        await session.commit()


async def _delete_test_data(
    session_factory,
    *,
    user_id,
) -> None:
    async with session_factory() as session:
        await session.execute(
            text(
                """
                DELETE FROM academy_notification_deliveries
                WHERE user_id = :user_id
                """
            ),
            {"user_id": user_id},
        )

        await session.execute(
            text(
                """
                DELETE FROM users
                WHERE id = :user_id
                """
            ),
            {"user_id": user_id},
        )

        await session.commit()


async def _get_delivery(
    session_factory,
    *,
    user_id,
):
    async with session_factory() as session:
        result = await session.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT
                    status,
                    attempt_count,
                    provider_message_id,
                    last_error,
                    sent_at
                FROM academy_notification_deliveries
                WHERE user_id = :user_id
                  AND notification_type = :notification_type
                """
            ),
            {
                "user_id": user_id,
                "notification_type": (
                    AcademyOneWeekWorkflow.NOTIFICATION_TYPE
                ),
            },
        )

        return result.first()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_real_postgresql_failure_is_persisted_then_retry_succeeds():
    """
    Real PostgreSQL retry lifecycle.

    First run:
        claim -> notification failure -> FAILED

    Second run:
        retry -> notification success -> SUCCEEDED
    """

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    session_factory = create_async_session_factory(engine)

    user_id = uuid4()

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    created_at = now - timedelta(
        days=7,
        hours=12,
    )

    email = (
        f"academy-retry-{user_id}"
        "@example.invalid"
    )

    username = (
        f"academy_retry_{user_id.hex[:12]}"
    )

    notifications = ControlledNotifications()

    try:
        await _create_test_user(
            session_factory,
            user_id=user_id,
            email=email,
            username=username,
            created_at=created_at,
        )

        workflow = AcademyOneWeekWorkflow(
            users=PostgreSQLAcademyUserQuery(
                session_factory,
            ),
            deliveries=PostgreSQLAcademyDeliveryRepository(
                session_factory,
            ),
            notifications=notifications,
            clock=lambda: now,
        )

        first_result = await workflow.run()

        assert first_result["candidates"] == 1
        assert first_result["attempted"] == 1
        assert first_result["succeeded"] == 0
        assert first_result["failed"] == 1
        assert first_result["skipped"] == 0

        delivery_after_failure = await _get_delivery(
            session_factory,
            user_id=user_id,
        )

        assert delivery_after_failure is not None
        assert delivery_after_failure.status == "FAILED"
        assert delivery_after_failure.attempt_count == 1
        assert (
            delivery_after_failure.provider_message_id
            is None
        )
        assert (
            delivery_after_failure.last_error
            == "controlled integration failure"
        )
        assert delivery_after_failure.sent_at is None

        second_result = await workflow.run()

        assert second_result["candidates"] == 1
        assert second_result["attempted"] == 1
        assert second_result["succeeded"] == 1
        assert second_result["failed"] == 0
        assert second_result["skipped"] == 0

        assert notifications.calls == 2

        delivery_after_retry = await _get_delivery(
            session_factory,
            user_id=user_id,
        )

        assert delivery_after_retry is not None
        assert delivery_after_retry.status == "SUCCEEDED"
        assert delivery_after_retry.attempt_count == 2
        assert (
            delivery_after_retry.provider_message_id
            == "retry-integration-message-id"
        )
        assert delivery_after_retry.last_error is None
        assert delivery_after_retry.sent_at is not None

    finally:
        await _delete_test_data(
            session_factory,
            user_id=user_id,
        )

        await engine.dispose()

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\academy\test_academy_scheduler_invocation.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_apscheduler_adapter import (
    CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def test_academy_scheduler_job_points_to_workflow_run():
    """
    The Academy scheduler job must point to the actual async
    AcademyOneWeekWorkflow.run() method.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    workflow_service = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow_service",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow.run = AsyncMock(
        name="academy_workflow_run",
        return_value={
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        },
    )

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=workflow_service,
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_calls = [
        call
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
        if call.kwargs.get("id") == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    ]

    assert len(academy_calls) == 1

    academy_call = academy_calls[0]

    assert academy_call.args[0] is academy_workflow.run
    assert academy_call.kwargs["trigger"] == "cron"
    assert academy_call.kwargs["hour"] == 8
    assert academy_call.kwargs["minute"] == 15


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_registered_academy_job_can_invoke_workflow_without_external_delivery():
    """
    Invoke the registered callback directly.

    This simulates APScheduler executing the job while avoiding
    production scheduler startup and all external delivery.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    workflow_service = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow_service",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow.run = AsyncMock(
        name="academy_workflow_run",
        return_value={
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        },
    )

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=workflow_service,
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_call = next(
        call
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
        if call.kwargs.get("id") == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    )

    registered_callback = academy_call.args[0]

    result = await registered_callback()

    assert result == {
        "candidates": 0,
        "attempted": 0,
        "succeeded": 0,
        "failed": 0,
        "skipped": 0,
    }

    academy_workflow.run.assert_awaited_once_with()

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime import (
    CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_shared_scheduler_lifecycle_starts_and_stops_cleanly():
    """
    The shared scheduler runtime must start and stop cleanly.

    Academy is already injected into the existing scheduler boundary.
    """

    adapter = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_adapter",
    )

    adapter.start = MagicMock(
        name="start",
    )

    adapter.shutdown = MagicMock(
        name="shutdown",
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        scheduler_adapter=adapter,
    )

    await runtime.start()

    adapter.start.assert_called_once()

    await runtime.stop()

    adapter.shutdown.assert_called_once()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_scheduler_lifecycle_does_not_execute_academy_workflow():
    """
    Starting/stopping the scheduler lifecycle must not directly execute
    the Academy workflow.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    scheduler.running = False

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    adapter = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_adapter",
    )

    adapter.scheduler = scheduler
    adapter.academy_one_week_workflow = academy_workflow

    adapter.start = MagicMock(
        name="start",
    )

    adapter.shutdown = MagicMock(
        name="shutdown",
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        scheduler_adapter=adapter,
    )

    await runtime.start()
    await runtime.stop()

    academy_workflow.run.assert_not_called()

    scheduler infrastructure itself.
    """

    adapter = MagicMock(
        name="existing_scheduler_adapter",
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        scheduler_adapter=adapter,
    )

    assert runtime.scheduler_adapter is adapter

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory import (
    create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def _create_controlled_scheduler():
    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    scheduler.running = False

    return scheduler


def test_academy_workflow_is_injected_into_existing_scheduler():
    """
    Academy workflow must be injected into the existing scheduler
    runtime boundary.
    """

    scheduler = _create_controlled_scheduler()

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=scheduler,
    ):

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=MagicMock(
                name="cybersecurity_workflow",
            ),
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    assert adapter.scheduler is scheduler

    assert (
        adapter.academy_one_week_workflow
        is academy_workflow
    )


def test_academy_job_registration_does_not_start_scheduler():
    """
    Academy registration must add exactly one Academy job and must not
    start or execute the scheduler.
    """

    scheduler = _create_controlled_scheduler()

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=scheduler,
    ):

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=MagicMock(
                name="cybersecurity_workflow",
            ),
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_calls = [
        call
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
        if call.kwargs.get("id")
        == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    ]

    assert len(academy_calls) == 1

    academy_call = academy_calls[0]

    assert (
        academy_call.args[0]
        is academy_workflow.run
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["trigger"]
        == "cron"
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["hour"]
        == adapter.academy_one_week_hour
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["minute"]
        == adapter.academy_one_week_minute
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["id"]
        == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["replace_existing"]
        is True
    )

    assert scheduler.start.called is False

    assert scheduler.running is False

    assert academy_workflow.run.called is False


def test_academy_and_cybersecurity_jobs_share_one_scheduler():
    """
    Academy and existing cybersecurity jobs must be registered on
    the same scheduler instance.
    """

    scheduler = _create_controlled_scheduler()

    cybersecurity_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=scheduler,
    ):

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=cybersecurity_workflow,
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    assert adapter.scheduler is scheduler

    adapter.register_jobs()

    registered_ids = {
        call.kwargs.get("id")
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
    }

    assert (
        "cybersecurity_daily_report"
        in registered_ids
    )

    assert (
        "cybersecurity_weekly_report"
        in registered_ids
    )

    assert (
        "academy_one_week_milestone"
        in registered_ids
    )

    assert scheduler.start.called is False
    assert scheduler.running is False

    assert cybersecurity_workflow.create_daily_report.called is False
    assert cybersecurity_workflow.create_weekly_report.called is False
    assert academy_workflow.run.called is False

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\academy\test_academy_scheduler_smoke.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory import (
    create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_shared_scheduler_start_registers_academy_and_stops_cleanly():
    """
    Controlled real APScheduler lifecycle smoke test.

    Verifies:
    - one shared scheduler;
    - Academy job registration;
    - existing cybersecurity jobs registration;
    - scheduler starts inside a running asyncio event loop;
    - scheduler becomes running;
    - Academy workflow is not executed;
    - shutdown is invoked cleanly.

    No real email or Telegram delivery is performed.
    """

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
        workflow_service=MagicMock(
            name="cybersecurity_workflow",
        ),
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    try:
        adapter.register_jobs()

        jobs = adapter.scheduler.get_jobs()

        job_ids = {
            job.id
            for job in jobs
        }

        assert (
            "academy_one_week_milestone"
            in job_ids
        )

        assert (
            "cybersecurity_daily_report"
            in job_ids
        )

        assert (
            "cybersecurity_weekly_report"
            in job_ids
        )

        assert adapter.scheduler.running is False

        adapter.start()

        assert adapter.scheduler.running is True

        # Starting the scheduler must not execute Academy immediately.
        assert academy_workflow.run.call_count == 0

    finally:
        adapter.shutdown()

        # Give the asyncio scheduler event loop one opportunity to
        # process its shutdown state.
        await asyncio.sleep(0)

    # The important lifecycle contract is that shutdown was invoked
    # and no Academy execution occurred.
    assert academy_workflow.run.call_count == 0

from __future__ import annotations

import os

import pytest
from dotenv import load_dotenv

from app.application.announcements.execution.audit_adapter import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionAudit,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_composition import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionComposition,
    create_durable_publication_execution_composition,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionService,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.publication_audit_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository,
)


def _load_repository_environment() -> None:
    """Load the repository .env before creating the canonical DB boundary."""

    load_dotenv()

    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip(
            "DATABASE_URL is not configured for PostgreSQL integration tests."
        )


class RecordingPublisher:
    async def publish(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        return "provider-test-001"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws22_composition_uses_canonical_shared_persistence() -> None:
    _load_repository_environment()

    composition = create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
        publisher=RecordingPublisher(),
    )

    try:
        assert isinstance(
            composition,
            DurablePublicationExecutionComposition,
        )

        assert isinstance(
            composition.service,
            DurablePublicationExecutionService,
        )

        assert isinstance(
            composition.execution_repository,
            SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository,
        )

        assert isinstance(
            composition.audit_repository,
            SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository,
        )

        assert isinstance(
            composition.audit,
            DurablePublicationExecutionAudit,
        )

        # Critical architectural invariant:
        # both WS-22 durable repositories use the canonical shared
        # async session factory.
        assert (
            composition.execution_repository._session_factory
            is composition.session_factory
        )

        assert (
            composition.audit_repository._session_factory
            is composition.session_factory
        )

    finally:
        await composition.dispose()

async def test_ws22_composition_rejects_missing_publisher() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="publisher cannot be None"):
        create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
            publisher=None,
        )

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

import pytest
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from sqlalchemy import delete, select

from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    PublicationExecutionRequest,
    PublicationExecutionStatus,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_composition import (
    create_durable_publication_execution_composition,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.publication_audit_repository import (
    PublicationAuditEventModel,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository import (
    PublicationExecutionRecordModel,
)


def _load_repository_environment() -> None:
    load_dotenv()

    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip(
            "DATABASE_URL is not configured for PostgreSQL integration tests."
        )


class RecordingPublisher:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[dict] = []

    async def publish(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        self.calls.append(kwargs)
        return "provider-ws22-3-5-4-001"


@dataclass
class ApprovedPublication:
    id: UUID
    title: str = "WS-22.3.5.4 test"
    content: str = "Durable composition integration test."
    status: str = "approved"

    def begin_publish(self) -> None:
        pass

    def publish_success(
        self,
        provider_message_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.status = "published"

    def mark_failure(self, error_code: str) -> None:
        self.status = "failed"


async def _cleanup(
    composition,
    *,
    idempotency_key: str,
    publication_id: UUID,
) -> None:
    async with composition.session_factory() as session:
        await session.execute(
            delete(PublicationAuditEventModel).where(
                PublicationAuditEventModel.publication_id
                == str(publication_id)
            )
        )

        await session.execute(
            delete(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                == idempotency_key
            )
        )

        await session.commit()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_composition_executes_and_persists_execution_and_audit() -> None:
    _load_repository_environment()

    publisher = RecordingPublisher()

    composition = create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
        publisher=publisher,
    )

    publication_id = uuid4()
    idempotency_key = f"ws22-3-5-4-{uuid4()}"
    correlation_id = f"corr-{uuid4()}"

    request = PublicationExecutionRequest(
        publication_id=publication_id,
        idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
        actor="ws22-3-5-4-test",
        correlation_id=correlation_id,
    )

    publication = ApprovedPublication(id=publication_id)

    try:
        result = await composition.service.execute(
            publication=publication,
            request=request,
        )

        assert result.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
        assert result.provider_message_id == "provider-ws22-3-5-4-001"
        assert len(publisher.calls) == 1

        async with composition.session_factory() as session:
            execution = await session.scalar(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            assert execution is not None
            assert execution.idempotency_key == idempotency_key
            assert execution.correlation_id == correlation_id
            assert execution.provider_message_id == (
                "provider-ws22-3-5-4-001"
            )

            audit_events = (
                await session.scalars(
                    select(PublicationAuditEventModel)
                    .where(
                        PublicationAuditEventModel.publication_id
                        == str(publication_id)
                    )
                    .order_by(PublicationAuditEventModel.id)
                )
            ).all()

            assert len(audit_events) == 1
            assert audit_events[0].event_type == "published"
            assert audit_events[0].actor == "ws22-3-5-4-test"
            assert audit_events[0].correlation_id == correlation_id
            assert audit_events[0].provider_message_id == (
                "provider-ws22-3-5-4-001"
            )

    finally:
        await _cleanup(
            composition,
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            publication_id=publication_id,
        )
        await composition.dispose()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_composition_replays_without_second_provider_call() -> None:
    _load_repository_environment()

    publisher = RecordingPublisher()

    composition = create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
        publisher=publisher,
    )

    publication_id = uuid4()
    idempotency_key = f"ws22-3-5-4-replay-{uuid4()}"
    correlation_id = f"corr-{uuid4()}"

    request = PublicationExecutionRequest(
        publication_id=publication_id,
        idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
        actor="ws22-3-5-4-test",
        correlation_id=correlation_id,
    )

    try:
        first = await composition.service.execute(
            publication=ApprovedPublication(id=publication_id),
            request=request,
        )

        second = await composition.service.execute(
            publication=ApprovedPublication(id=publication_id),
            request=request,
        )

        assert first.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
        assert second.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY

        assert second.provider_message_id == first.provider_message_id
        assert len(publisher.calls) == 1

    finally:
        await _cleanup(
            composition,
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            publication_id=publication_id,
        )
        await composition.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

import pytest
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from sqlalchemy import delete, select

from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    PublicationExecutionRequest,
    PublicationExecutionStatus,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_composition import (
    create_durable_publication_execution_composition,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.publication_audit_repository import (
    PublicationAuditEventModel,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository import (
    PublicationExecutionRecordModel,
)


def _load_repository_environment() -> None:
    load_dotenv()

    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip(
            "DATABASE_URL is not configured for PostgreSQL integration tests."
        )


class RecordingPublisher:
    def __init__(self, *, fail: bool = False) -> None:
        self.fail = fail
        self.calls: list[dict] = []

    async def publish(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        self.calls.append(kwargs)

        if self.fail:
            error = RuntimeError("simulated provider timeout")
            error.error_code = "PROVIDER_TIMEOUT"
            raise error

        return "provider-ws22-3-5-5-retry-success"


@dataclass
class ApprovedPublication:
    id: UUID
    title: str = "WS-22.3.5.5 test"
    content: str = "Failure and retry integration test."
    status: str = "approved"

    def begin_publish(self) -> None:
        pass

    def publish_success(
        self,
        provider_message_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.status = "published"

    def mark_failure(self, error_code: str) -> None:
        self.status = "failed"


async def _cleanup(
    composition,
    *,
    idempotency_key: str,
    publication_id: UUID,
) -> None:
    async with composition.session_factory() as session:
        await session.execute(
            delete(PublicationAuditEventModel).where(
                PublicationAuditEventModel.publication_id
                == str(publication_id)
            )
        )

        await session.execute(
            delete(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                == idempotency_key
            )
        )

        await session.commit()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_failure_is_durable_and_retry_succeeds() -> None:
    _load_repository_environment()

    publication_id = uuid4()
    idempotency_key = f"ws22-3-5-5-{uuid4()}"
    correlation_id = f"corr-{uuid4()}"

    request = PublicationExecutionRequest(
        publication_id=publication_id,
        idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
        actor="ws22-3-5-5-test",
        correlation_id=correlation_id,
    )

    composition_1 = create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
        publisher=RecordingPublisher(fail=True),
    )

    try:
        failed = await composition_1.service.execute(
            publication=ApprovedPublication(id=publication_id),
            request=request,
        )

        assert failed.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.FAILED
        assert failed.error_code == "PROVIDER_TIMEOUT"

        async with composition_1.session_factory() as session:
            execution = await session.scalar(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            assert execution is not None
            assert execution.status == "failed"
            assert execution.attempt == 1
            assert execution.error_code == "PROVIDER_TIMEOUT"
            assert execution.provider_message_id is None

            audit_events = (
                await session.scalars(
                    select(PublicationAuditEventModel)
                    .where(
                        PublicationAuditEventModel.publication_id
                        == str(publication_id)
                    )
                    .order_by(PublicationAuditEventModel.id)
                )
            ).all()

            assert len(audit_events) == 1
            assert audit_events[0].event_type == "publish_failed"
            assert audit_events[0].error_code == "PROVIDER_TIMEOUT"
            assert audit_events[0].correlation_id == correlation_id

    finally:
        await composition_1.dispose()

    # Simulate application restart: completely new composition.
    publisher_2 = RecordingPublisher(fail=False)

    composition_2 = create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
        publisher=publisher_2,
    )

    try:
        retry = await composition_2.service.execute(
            publication=ApprovedPublication(id=publication_id),
            request=request,
        )

        assert retry.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
        assert retry.provider_message_id == (
            "provider-ws22-3-5-5-retry-success"
        )
        assert len(publisher_2.calls) == 1

        async with composition_2.session_factory() as session:
            execution = await session.scalar(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            assert execution is not None
            assert execution.status == "succeeded"
            assert execution.attempt == 2
            assert execution.error_code is None
            assert execution.provider_message_id == (
                "provider-ws22-3-5-5-retry-success"
            )

            audit_events = (
                await session.scalars(
                    select(PublicationAuditEventModel)
                    .where(
                        PublicationAuditEventModel.publication_id
                        == str(publication_id)
                    )
                    .order_by(PublicationAuditEventModel.id)
                )
            ).all()

            assert len(audit_events) == 2
            assert audit_events[0].event_type == "publish_failed"
            assert audit_events[1].event_type == "published"

    finally:
        await _cleanup(
            composition_2,
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            publication_id=publication_id,
        )
        await composition_2.dispose()

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\announcements\test_ws23_1_3_persistence_composition.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, status
from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient

from app.api.announcement_operations_approval import (
    announcement_publish_permission,
    create_announcement_operations_approval_router,
)
from app.application.announcements.approval.async_approval_service import (
    AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
)
from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
from app.domain.announcements.template_models import (
    PublicationStatus,
    PublicationType,
)


class FakeAsyncRepository:
    def __init__(self, publication: Publication | None) -> None:
        self.publication = publication
        self.saved: list[Publication] = []

    async def get_async(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
        if (
            self.publication is not None
            and self.publication.publication_id == publication_id
        ):
            return self.publication
        return None

    async def save_async(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        self.saved.append(publication)
        self.publication = publication


class AuditRecorder:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.events: list[dict] = []

    async def record_approval(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        self.events.append(kwargs)


def make_publication() -> Publication:
    return Publication(
        publication_id="ws23-1-3-persistence-test",
        template_id="beta-update",
        template_version=1,
        publication_type=PublicationType.BETA_UPDATE,
        title="Beta update",
        content="Test announcement",
        status=PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL,
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )


def make_principal(user_id: str = "operator-test") -> SimpleNamespace:
    return SimpleNamespace(user_id=user_id)


async def deny_announcement_publish_permission() -> None:
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
        detail="Permission denied.",
    )


async def allow_announcement_publish_permission() -> SimpleNamespace:
    return make_principal()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_async_approval_persists_state_and_audit_contract() -> None:
    repository = FakeAsyncRepository(make_publication())
    audit = AuditRecorder()

    service = AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService(
        publications=repository,
        audit=audit,
    )

    result = await service.approve(
        publication_id="ws23-1-3-persistence-test",
        actor="admin-test",
    )

    assert result.publication.status is PublicationStatus.APPROVED
    assert len(repository.saved) == 1
    assert len(audit.events) == 1
    assert audit.events[0]["actor"] == "admin-test"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_api_denies_unauthorized_approval() -> None:
    repository = FakeAsyncRepository(make_publication())
    audit = AuditRecorder()

    service = AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService(
        publications=repository,
        audit=audit,
    )

    app = FastAPI()

    router = create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
        approval_service=service,
    )

    app.include_router(router)

    # Override the exact authorization dependency used by the route.
    # This keeps the test isolated from the application's auth_repository
    # composition while exercising the production authorization boundary.
    app.dependency_overrides[announcement_publish_permission] = (
        deny_announcement_publish_permission
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=ASGITransport(app=app),
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:
        response = await client.post(
            "/api/announcement-operations/publications/"
            "ws23-1-3-persistence-test/approve",
            json={"actor": "admin-test"},
        )

    assert response.status_code == 403

    assert repository.publication is not None
    assert (
        repository.publication.status
        is PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL
    )

    assert repository.saved == []
    assert audit.events == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_api_authorized_approval_uses_authenticated_actor() -> None:
    repository = FakeAsyncRepository(make_publication())
    audit = AuditRecorder()

    service = AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService(
        publications=repository,
        audit=audit,
    )

    app = FastAPI()

    router = create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
        approval_service=service,
    )

    app.include_router(router)

    app.dependency_overrides[announcement_publish_permission] = (
        allow_announcement_publish_permission
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=ASGITransport(app=app),
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:
        response = await client.post(
            "/api/announcement-operations/publications/"
            "ws23-1-3-persistence-test/approve",
            json={"actor": "attacker-controlled-actor"},
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    assert repository.publication is not None
    assert repository.publication.status is PublicationStatus.APPROVED

    assert len(repository.saved) == 1
    assert len(audit.events) == 1

    assert audit.events[0]["actor"] == "operator-test"
    assert audit.events[0]["actor"] != "attacker-controlled-actor"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_invalid_transition_is_rejected() -> None:
    publication = make_publication()
    publication.status = PublicationStatus.DRAFT

    repository = FakeAsyncRepository(publication)
    audit = AuditRecorder()

    service = AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService(
        publications=repository,
        audit=audit,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid publication transition"):
        await service.approve(
            publication_id=publication.publication_id,
            actor="admin-test",
        )

    assert repository.saved == []
    assert audit.events == []

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\announcements\test_ws23_1_4_telegram_composition_adapter.py

from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from app.infrastructure.announcements.execution.telegram_publication_publisher_adapter import (
    TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter,
)


class FakeControlledPublisher:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[dict] = []

    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        channel_id: str,
        text: str,
        announcement_type: str,
    ):
        self.calls.append(
            {
                "channel_id": channel_id,
                "text": text,
                "announcement_type": announcement_type,
            }
        )

        class Record:
            message_id = "telegram-message-123"

        return Record()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws23_1_4_adapter_delegates_to_controlled_publisher() -> None:
    controlled = FakeControlledPublisher()

    adapter = TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter(
        publisher=controlled,
        channel_id="-1001234567890",
        announcement_type="beta_update",
    )

    message_id = await adapter.publish(
        publication_id=uuid4(),
        title="Beta 1",
        content="The beta is ready.",
        correlation_id="corr-1",
        idempotency_key="idem-1",
    )

    assert message_id == "telegram-message-123"

    assert controlled.calls == [
        {
            "channel_id": "-1001234567890",
            "text": "*Beta 1*\n\nThe beta is ready.",
            "announcement_type": "beta_update",
        }
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws23_1_4_adapter_rejects_empty_title() -> None:
    controlled = FakeControlledPublisher()

    adapter = TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter(
        publisher=controlled,
        channel_id="-1001234567890",
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="title cannot be empty"):
        await adapter.publish(
            publication_id=uuid4(),
            title=" ",
            content="content",
            correlation_id="corr",
            idempotency_key="idem",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws23_1_4_adapter_rejects_empty_content() -> None:
    controlled = FakeControlledPublisher()

    adapter = TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter(
        publisher=controlled,
        channel_id="-1001234567890",
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="content cannot be empty"):
        await adapter.publish(
            publication_id=uuid4(),
            title="Beta 1",
            content=" ",
            correlation_id="corr",
            idempotency_key="idem",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws23_1_4_adapter_rejects_empty_correlation_id() -> None:
    controlled = FakeControlledPublisher()

    adapter = TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter(
        publisher=controlled,
        channel_id="-1001234567890",
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="correlation_id cannot be empty"):
        await adapter.publish(
            publication_id=uuid4(),
            title="Beta 1",
            content="content",
            correlation_id=" ",
            idempotency_key="idem",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws23_1_4_adapter_rejects_empty_idempotency_key() -> None:
    controlled = FakeControlledPublisher()

    adapter = TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter(
        publisher=controlled,
        channel_id="-1001234567890",
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="idempotency_key cannot be empty"):

            correlation_id="corr",
            idempotency_key=" ",
        )

        ValueError,
        match="BETA1_ANNOUNCEMENT_CHANNEL_ID is not configured",

        )

from app.api.platform_intelligence_review import create_platform_intelligence_review_router
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_source import (
    UnconfiguredPlatformIntelligenceReviewSource,
)


def test_router_factory_requires_api():
    try:
        create_platform_intelligence_review_router(api=None)
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert str(exc) == "api is required."
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Expected ValueError")


def test_router_exposes_read_only_review_endpoints():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(
        source=UnconfiguredPlatformIntelligenceReviewSource()
    )
    router = create_platform_intelligence_review_router(api=api)
    paths = {route.path for route in router.routes}

    assert "/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews" in paths
    assert "/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews/{review_item_id}" in paths
    assert "/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/escalations" in paths

"""
P1.6.4D Operations Center composition contract tests.

Protects the Operations Center composition boundary for provider-usage
dashboard wiring.

The composition must:
- remain infrastructure-agnostic;
- accept ProviderUsageDashboardService through dependency injection;
- wire the injected service into the Operations Center composition;
- remain constructible without requiring database configuration.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import Mock

from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_composition,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_usage_dashboard_service import (
    ProviderUsageDashboardService,
)


def test_p1_6_4d_default_composition_is_constructible_without_database_configuration(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Operations Center composition must not create its own database infrastructure."""

    monkeypatch.delenv("DATABASE_URL", raising=False)

    composition = create_operations_center_composition()

    assert composition is not None


def test_p1_6_4d_injected_provider_usage_dashboard_service_is_preserved() -> None:
    """The injected provider-usage dashboard service must remain part of the composition."""

    provider_usage_dashboard_service = Mock(
        spec=ProviderUsageDashboardService,
    )

    composition = create_operations_center_composition(
        provider_usage_dashboard_service=provider_usage_dashboard_service,
    )

    assert (
        composition.provider_usage_dashboard_service
        is provider_usage_dashboard_service
    )


def test_p1_6_4d_composition_has_no_database_infrastructure_attributes() -> None:
    """Database infrastructure must remain owned by application bootstrap."""

    composition = create_operations_center_composition()

    assert not hasattr(composition, "provider_usage_engine")
    assert not hasattr(composition, "provider_usage_session_factory")
    assert not hasattr(composition, "provider_usage_repository")

    Verify the complete composition chain works:

        Composition
              |
              ↓
        Application Service
              |
              ↓
        Repository
              |
              ↓
        Stored Invitation
    """


    service = (
        create_voice_call_invitation_service()
    )


    invitation = (
        service.create_invitation(
            voice_call_id="vc-001",
            learner_id="learner-001",
            character_id="yeti",
            channel="notification",
        )
    )


    assert invitation.id.startswith(
        "inv-"
    )


    stored = (
        service.get_invitation(
            invitation_id=invitation.id,
        )
    )


    assert stored is not None


    assert stored.id == (
        invitation.id
    )


    assert stored.voice_call_id == (
        "vc-001"
    )

        self,

        build_voice_call_event_service()
    )


    event = (
        service.record_event(
            voice_call_id="vc-001",
            learner_id="learner-001",
            character_id="yeti",
            event_type=(
                VoiceCallEventType.CREATED

    service = (
        build_voice_call_event_service()
    )


    service.record_event(
        voice_call_id="vc-002",
        learner_id="learner-002",
        character_id="alix",
        event_type=(
            VoiceCallEventType.ACCEPTED
        ),
    )


    events = (
        service.get_events(
            voice_call_id="vc-002",
        )
    )


    assert len(events) >= 1


    assert events[-1].event_type == (
        VoiceCallEventType.ACCEPTED

from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.application.composition import beta_telegram_composition as composition


class FakePrivateChatBridge:
    def __init__(self, *, private_chat_service_factory):
        self.private_chat_service_factory = private_chat_service_factory


class FakeConversationBoundary:
    last_kwargs = None

    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        type(self).last_kwargs = kwargs


class FakeConversationService:
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        self.kwargs = kwargs


class FakeBetaAccessService:
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        self.kwargs = kwargs


class FakePrivateChatGate:
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        self.kwargs = kwargs


class FakeStartHandler:
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        self.kwargs = kwargs


class FakeAdapter:
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        self.kwargs = kwargs


class FakeRuntime:
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        self.kwargs = kwargs


@pytest.mark.parametrize("factory_value", [lambda: object()])
def test_create_beta_telegram_composition_wires_private_chat_bridge(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    factory_value,
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        composition,
        "BetaAccessService",
        FakeBetaAccessService,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        composition,
        "TelegramConversationService",
        FakeConversationService,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        composition,
        "TelegramPrivateChatBridge",
        FakePrivateChatBridge,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        composition,
        "BetaTelegramConversationBoundary",
        FakeConversationBoundary,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        composition,
        "BetaPrivateChatGate",
        FakePrivateChatGate,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        composition,
        "BetaTelegramStartHandler",
        FakeStartHandler,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        composition,
        "BetaTelegramAdapter",
        FakeAdapter,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        composition,
        "TelegramBotRuntime",
        FakeRuntime,
    )

    runtime = object()
    beta_world_access_service = object()
    language_detector = object()
    world_resolver = object()
    language_policy = object()

    result = composition.create_beta_telegram_composition(
        user_repository=object(),
        token_repository=object(),
        binding_repository=object(),
        conversation_event_repository=object(),
        privacy_identifier_service=object(),
        conversation_event_validator=object(),
        beta_world_access_service=beta_world_access_service,
        conversation_runtime=runtime,
        world_resolver=world_resolver,
        language_response_policy=language_policy,
        language_detector=language_detector,
        private_chat_service_factory=factory_value,
    )

    bridge = result.private_chat_bridge

    assert bridge is not None
    assert bridge.private_chat_service_factory is factory_value

    boundary_kwargs = FakeConversationBoundary.last_kwargs
    assert boundary_kwargs is not None
    assert boundary_kwargs["private_chat_bridge"] is bridge

    assert result.conversation_boundary is not None
    assert result.private_chat_bridge is bridge

from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from app.application.learning_assessment.questionnaire_runtime import (
    QuestionnaireRuntime,
)
from app.application.learning_assessment.questionnaire_runtime_composition import (
    create_questionnaire_runtime,
)


def test_questionnaire_runtime_composition_requires_session_factory():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="session_factory is required"):
        create_questionnaire_runtime(session_factory=None)


def test_questionnaire_runtime_composition_returns_runtime():
    session_factory = Mock()

    runtime = create_questionnaire_runtime(
        session_factory=session_factory,
    )

    assert isinstance(runtime, QuestionnaireRuntime)

    assessment_service = runtime._assessment_service

    assert assessment_service._questions._session_factory is session_factory
    assert assessment_service._attempts._session_factory is session_factory
    assert assessment_service._events._session_factory is session_factory
    assert assessment_service._skill_states._session_factory is session_factory

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import Mock

from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    OperationsCenterComposition,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_usage_dashboard_service import (
    ProviderUsageDashboardService,
)


def test_operations_center_composition_accepts_usage_service_without_database_creation(
    monkeypatch,
):
    import app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition as module

    def fail_database_creation():
        raise AssertionError(
            "Operations Center composition must not create a database engine."
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "create_database_engine", fail_database_creation, raising=False)

    service = Mock(spec=ProviderUsageDashboardService)

    composition = OperationsCenterComposition(
        provider_usage_dashboard_service=service,
    )

    assert composition.provider_usage_dashboard_service is service


def test_operations_center_router_can_be_composed_with_injected_usage_service():
    import app.api.operations_center as module

    service = Mock(spec=ProviderUsageDashboardService)
    composition = OperationsCenterComposition(
        provider_usage_dashboard_service=service,
    )

    router = module.create_operations_center_router(composition=composition)

    paths = {route.path for route in router.routes}

    assert "/api/operations-center/provider-usage" in paths
    assert "/api/operations-center/dashboard" in paths
    assert "/api/operations-center/functionality-health" in paths


def test_importing_operations_center_module_does_not_require_database_url(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("DATABASE_URL", raising=False)

    sys.modules.pop("backend.app.api.operations_center", None)

    module = importlib.import_module("backend.app.api.operations_center")

    assert module.router is not None

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\application\operations_center\test_platform_intelligence_review_api.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_source import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource,
)


class FakeSource(PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource):
    def __init__(self, items):
        self.items = tuple(items)

    def list_reviews(self):
        return self.items

    def get_review(self, review_item_id):
        return next(
            (item for item in self.items if item.review_item_id == review_item_id),
            None,
        )

    def list_escalations(self):
        return tuple(
            item
            for item in self.items
            if getattr(
                getattr(item, "recommendation_status", None),
                "value",
                getattr(item, "recommendation_status", None),
            )
            == "RECOMMENDED"
        )


def projection(*, recommended=False):
    return SimpleNamespace(
        review_item_id="review-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        risk_assessment_id="assessment-1",
        scope="platform",
        risk_level=SimpleNamespace(value="HIGH"),
        risk_score=0.8,
        priority=SimpleNamespace(value="HIGH"),
        review_status=SimpleNamespace(value="IN_REVIEW"),
        decision_status=SimpleNamespace(value="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW"),
        quality_gate_status=SimpleNamespace(value="PASSED"),
        evidence_references=("e1",),
        historical_references=("h1",),
        policy_id="risk-policy",
        policy_version="1.0",
        created_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        assigned_reviewer="reviewer-1",
        assigned_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        review_due_at=None,
        rationale="Governed review required.",
        recommendation_status=(
            SimpleNamespace(value="RECOMMENDED") if recommended else None
        ),
        recommendation_route=(
            SimpleNamespace(value="RISK_OWNER") if recommended else None
        ),
        recommendation_reasons=("risk_level=HIGH",) if recommended else (),
        recommendation_id="esc-1" if recommended else None,
        metadata={"sensitive": "must-not-leak"},
    )


def test_list_serializes_only_approved_projection_fields():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=FakeSource([projection()]))

    result = api.list_reviews()

    assert result["status"] == "ready"
    assert result["count"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["risk_decision_id"] == "decision-1"
    assert "metadata" not in result["items"][0]


def test_get_missing_returns_none():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=FakeSource([]))

    assert api.get_review("missing") is None


def test_escalations_only_include_recommended_items():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(
        source=FakeSource([projection(), projection(recommended=True)])
    )

    result = api.list_escalations()

    assert result["count"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["recommendation_status"] == "RECOMMENDED"


def test_unconfigured_source_is_explicitly_not_configured():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=None)

    with pytest.raises(PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured):
        api.list_reviews()

------------------------------------------------------------
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_composition,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
)


class Source:
    def list_reviews(self):
        return [
            SimpleNamespace(
                review_item_id="R1",
                risk_decision_id="D1",
                risk_assessment_id="A1",
                scope="platform",
                risk_level="HIGH",
                risk_score=0.8,
                priority="HIGH",
                review_status="PENDING",
                decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
                quality_gate_status="PASSED",
                evidence_references=("E1",),
                historical_references=("H1",),
                policy_id="P",
                policy_version="1",
                created_at=SimpleNamespace(
                    isoformat=lambda: "2026-09-22T00:00:00+00:00"
                ),
                assigned_reviewer=None,
                assigned_at=None,
                review_due_at=None,
                rationale="test",
                recommendation_status="RECOMMENDED",
                recommendation_route="HUMAN_REVIEW",
                recommendation_reasons=("risk",),
                recommendation_id="REC1",
            )
        ]

    def get_review(self, review_item_id):
        return (
            self.list_reviews()[0]
            if review_item_id == "R1"
            else None
        )

    def list_escalations(self):
        return self.list_reviews()


def test_operations_center_composition_exposes_pi_review_api():
    composition = create_operations_center_composition(
        platform_intelligence_review_source=Source()
    )

    assert isinstance(
        composition.platform_intelligence_review_api,
        PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
    )

    assert (
        composition.platform_intelligence_review_api
        .list_reviews()["count"]
        == 1
    )


def test_operations_center_composition_default_is_fail_closed():
    composition = create_operations_center_composition()

    assert composition.platform_intelligence_review_api.configured is False

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.provider_management.functionality_operational_control_service import (

from tests.application.composition.deterministic_language_detector import (

    Deterministic repository double.

    Production uses PostgreSQLFunctionalityControlRepository.

    def get_availability(

    return FunctionalityOperationalControlService(
        authorizer=FakeFunctionalityControlAuthorizer(),
        repository=FakeFunctionalityControlRepository(),

def test_telegram_runtime_composition_creates_runtime(
    monkeypatch,
):
    #
    # Test-only privacy configuration.
    #
    # Never use this value as a production secret.
    #
    monkeypatch.setenv(
        "PRIVACY_HASH_SECRET",
        "test-only-telegram-composition-secret",
    )

    #
    # Database dependency isolation.
    #

    class FakeEngine:
        pass

    class FakeSessionFactory:
        pass

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "backend.application.telegram_bootstrap.create_database_engine",
        lambda: FakeEngine(),
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "backend.application.telegram_bootstrap.create_session_factory",
        lambda engine: FakeSessionFactory(),
    )

    #
    # AI runtime isolation.
    #

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "backend.application.telegram_bootstrap.create_conversation_runtime",
        lambda: FakeConversationRuntime(),
    )

    #
    # Functionality-control isolation.
    #
    # The production composition requires this dependency explicitly.
    # The test supplies a deterministic repository-backed service rather
    # than weakening the production contract.
    #

    functionality_control_service = (
        make_functionality_control_service()
    )

    reset_telegram_runtime()

    try:
        runtime = create_telegram_runtime(
            functionality_control_service=(
                functionality_control_service
            ),
            language_detector=DeterministicLanguageDetector(),
        )

        assert runtime is not None

        composition = get_telegram_production_composition()

        assert composition is not None
        assert composition.conversation_boundary is not None

    finally:
        reset_telegram_runtime()

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\cybersecurity\test_academy_scheduler_registration.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_apscheduler_adapter import (
    CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter,
)


class FakeScheduler:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.jobs: list[dict] = []
        self.running = False

    def add_job(self, function, **kwargs):
        self.jobs.append(
            {
                "function": function,
                **kwargs,
            }
        )

    def start(self) -> None:
        self.running = True

    def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.running = False


class FakeCybersecurityWorkflow:
    async def create_daily_report(self):
        return {"type": "daily"}

    async def create_weekly_report(self):
        return {"type": "weekly"}


class FakeAcademyWorkflow:
    async def run(self):
        return {
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        }


def test_academy_workflow_is_registered_on_existing_scheduler() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()
    academy = FakeAcademyWorkflow()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    assert len(scheduler.jobs) == 3

    academy_jobs = [
        job
        for job in scheduler.jobs
        if job["id"] == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    ]

    assert len(academy_jobs) == 1

    job = academy_jobs[0]

    assert job["function"] == academy.run
    assert job["trigger"] == "cron"
    assert job["hour"] == 8
    assert job["minute"] == 15
    assert job["replace_existing"] is True


def test_existing_jobs_remain_registered() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
        academy_one_week_workflow=FakeAcademyWorkflow(),
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    job_ids = {
        job["id"]
        for job in scheduler.jobs
    }

    assert job_ids == {
        "cybersecurity_daily_report",
        "cybersecurity_weekly_report",
        "academy_one_week_milestone",
    }


def test_academy_registration_is_optional() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    assert len(scheduler.jobs) == 2

    job_ids = {
        job["id"]
        for job in scheduler.jobs
    }

    assert "academy_one_week_milestone" not in job_ids


def test_academy_schedule_is_configurable() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
        academy_one_week_workflow=FakeAcademyWorkflow(),
        academy_one_week_hour=9,
        academy_one_week_minute=30,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_job = next(
        job
        for job in scheduler.jobs
        if job["id"] == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    )

    assert academy_job["hour"] == 9
    assert academy_job["minute"] == 30


def test_invalid_academy_hour_is_rejected() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    try:
        CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
            scheduler=scheduler,
            workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
            academy_one_week_workflow=FakeAcademyWorkflow(),
            academy_one_week_hour=24,
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "academy_one_week_hour" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Expected ValueError")


def test_invalid_academy_minute_is_rejected() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    try:
        CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
            scheduler=scheduler,
            workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
            academy_one_week_workflow=FakeAcademyWorkflow(),
            academy_one_week_minute=60,
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "academy_one_week_minute" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Expected ValueError")

from __future__ import annotations

import os

import pytest

from app.application.health.health_result import HealthStatus
from app.application.health.production_readiness_service import (
    ProductionReadinessService,
)
from app.infrastructure.health.llm_provider_health_check import (
    LLMProviderHealthCheck,
)
from app.infrastructure.health.postgresql_health_check import (
    PostgreSQLHealthCheck,
)
from app.infrastructure.health.telegram_health_check import (
    TelegramHealthCheck,
)


class FakeConnection:
    def __init__(self, calls):
        self.calls = calls

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
        return False

    def execute(self, statement):
        self.calls.append(str(statement))


class FakeEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = []
        self.disposed = False

    def connect(self):
        return FakeConnection(self.calls)

    def dispose(self):
        self.disposed = True


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_postgresql_probe_is_read_only():
    engine = FakeEngine()

    result = await PostgreSQLHealthCheck(
        engine=engine
    ).check()

    assert result.status is HealthStatus.HEALTHY
    assert "SELECT 1" in engine.calls[0]
    assert engine.disposed is False


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_postgresql_failure_is_sanitized():
    class BrokenEngine:
        def connect(self):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "postgresql password=SECRET"
            )

    result = await PostgreSQLHealthCheck(
        engine=BrokenEngine()
    ).check()

    assert result.status is HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY
    assert result.details == {
        "reason": "database_unavailable"
    }
    assert "SECRET" not in str(result.details)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_telegram_missing_token_is_not_configured(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", raising=False)

    result = await TelegramHealthCheck().check()

    assert result.status is HealthStatus.NOT_CONFIGURED
    assert result.details == {"configured": False}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_telegram_probe_uses_get_me_only(monkeypatch):
    calls = []

    class FakeUser:
        id = 123

    class FakeBot:
        def __init__(self, token):
            assert token == "test-token"

        async def get_me(self):
            calls.append("get_me")
            return FakeUser()

        async def shutdown(self):
            calls.append("shutdown")

    monkeypatch.setenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", "test-token")

    result = await TelegramHealthCheck(
        bot_factory=FakeBot
    ).check()

    assert result.status is HealthStatus.HEALTHY
    assert calls == ["get_me", "shutdown"]
    assert "test-token" not in str(result.details)

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_provider_failure_is_sanitized():
    async def failing_get(_url):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "api_key=SUPER_SECRET"
        )

    result = await LLMProviderHealthCheck(
        provider="ollama",
        request_get=failing_get,
    ).check()

    assert result.status is HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY
    assert result.details == {
        "provider": "ollama",
        "reason": "provider_unavailable",
    }
    assert "SUPER_SECRET" not in str(result.details)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_readiness_runs_all_checks_after_failure():
    class HealthyCheck:
        name = "healthy"

        async def check(self):
            return {
                "name": self.name
            }

    class BrokenCheck:
        name = "broken"

        async def check(self):
            raise RuntimeError("secret")

    # Return proper results while proving aggregation continues.
    from app.application.health.health_result import HealthCheckResult

    class SafeBrokenCheck:
        name = "broken"

        async def check(self):
            raise RuntimeError("secret")

    class SafeHealthyCheck:
        name = "healthy"

        async def check(self):
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            )

    result = await ProductionReadinessService(
        checks=(
            SafeBrokenCheck(),
            SafeHealthyCheck(),
        )
    ).check()

    assert result.ready is False
    assert [item.name for item in result.checks] == [
        "broken",
        "healthy",
    ]
    assert result.checks[0].details == {
        "reason": "health_check_failed"
    }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_full_readiness_aggregates_postgres_llm_telegram(
    monkeypatch,
):
    monkeypatch.setenv(
        "TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN",
        "test-token",
    )

    class FakeUser:
        id = 1

    class FakeBot:
        def __init__(self, token):
            pass

        async def get_me(self):
            return FakeUser()

        async def shutdown(self):
            pass

    async def ollama_get(_url):
        return type(
            "Response",
            (),
            {"status_code": 200},
        )()

    engine = FakeEngine()

    service = ProductionReadinessService(
        checks=(
            PostgreSQLHealthCheck(engine=engine),
            LLMProviderHealthCheck(
                provider="ollama",
                request_get=ollama_get,
            ),
            TelegramHealthCheck(
                bot_factory=FakeBot
            ),
        )
    )

    result = await service.check()

    assert result.ready is True
    assert {
        item.name for item in result.checks
    } == {
        "postgresql",
        "llm_provider",
        "telegram",
    }
