from __future__ import annotations

import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _database_required() -> None:
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is required for PostgreSQL integration tests.")


async def test_claim_retry_candidate_is_atomic_and_reclaimable_after_lease_expiry() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-claim-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-{uuid4()}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-{uuid4()}",
            review_item_id=f"review-{uuid4()}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-{uuid4()}",
        )
        await repository.mark_failed(
            notification_key=key,
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
        )

        first_token = str(uuid4())
        first_claimed_at = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        first_expiry = first_claimed_at + timedelta(minutes=5)

        first = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=first_token,
            claimed_at=first_claimed_at,
            lease_expires_at=first_expiry,
        )
        assert first is True

        second = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=str(uuid4()),
            claimed_at=first_claimed_at + timedelta(seconds=1),
            lease_expires_at=first_expiry + timedelta(seconds=1),
        )
        assert second is False

        third_token = str(uuid4())
        third_claimed_at = first_expiry + timedelta(seconds=1)
        third = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=third_token,
            claimed_at=third_claimed_at,
            lease_expires_at=third_claimed_at + timedelta(minutes=5),
        )
        assert third is True

        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            row = (
                await session.execute(
                    text(
                        """
                        SELECT claim_token, claimed_at, lease_expires_at
                        FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                        WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                        """
                    ),
                    {"notification_key": key},
                )
            ).mappings().one()

        assert row["claim_token"] == third_token
        assert row["claimed_at"] == third_claimed_at
    finally:
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": key},
            )
            await session.commit()
        await engine.dispose()


async def test_claim_does_not_claim_succeeded_delivery() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-claim-success-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-{uuid4()}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-{uuid4()}",
            review_item_id=f"review-{uuid4()}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-{uuid4()}",
        )
        await repository.mark_succeeded(
            notification_key=key,
            provider_message_id="provider-message",
        )

        claimed = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=str(uuid4()),
            claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
            lease_expires_at=datetime(
                2026, 9, 22, 12, 5, tzinfo=timezone.utc
            ),
        )

        assert claimed is False
    finally:
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": key},
            )
            await session.commit()
        await engine.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from app.api.authentication import router as authentication_router
from app.main import create_application, lifespan


def test_authentication_router_defines_required_endpoints() -> None:
    """The canonical authentication router exposes all required endpoints."""

    route_paths = {
        route.path
        for route in authentication_router.routes
        if hasattr(route, "path")
    }

    assert route_paths == {
        "/auth/register",
        "/auth/login",
        "/auth/password-reset/request",
        "/auth/password-reset/confirm",
        "/auth/password-reset/context",
        "/auth/verify-email",
        "/auth/logout",
        "/auth/session",
    }


def test_create_application_composes_authentication_repository() -> None:
    """Application composition creates the auth repository from the canonical session factory."""

    fake_engine = MagicMock(name="auth_engine")
    fake_session_factory = MagicMock(name="auth_session_factory")

    with (
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_database_engine",
            return_value=fake_engine,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_session_factory",
            return_value=fake_session_factory,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository",
        ) as repository_factory,
        patch(
            "app.main.create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime",
            return_value=MagicMock(),
        ),
    ):
        application = create_application()

    repository_factory.assert_called_once_with(
        fake_session_factory,
    )

    assert application.state.auth_engine is fake_engine
    assert application.state.auth_repository is (
        repository_factory.return_value
    )


def test_create_application_composes_authentication_router() -> None:
    """Application composition explicitly registers the canonical auth router."""

    fake_engine = MagicMock(name="auth_engine")
    fake_session_factory = MagicMock(name="auth_session_factory")

    with (
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_database_engine",
            return_value=fake_engine,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_session_factory",
            return_value=fake_session_factory,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository",
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime",
            return_value=MagicMock(),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.FastAPI.include_router",
        ) as include_router,
    ):
        create_application()

    registered_routers = [
        call.args[0]
        for call in include_router.call_args_list
        if call.args
    ]

    assert authentication_router in registered_routers


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_authentication_engine_is_disposed_by_application_lifespan() -> None:
    """The application-owned PostgreSQL engine is disposed during shutdown."""

    application = MagicMock()

    scheduler_runtime = AsyncMock()
    auth_engine = AsyncMock()

    application.state.scheduler_runtime = scheduler_runtime
    application.state.auth_engine = auth_engine
    application.state.announcement_telegram_client = None

    async with lifespan(application):
        pass

    scheduler_runtime.start.assert_awaited_once()
    scheduler_runtime.stop.assert_awaited_once()
    auth_engine.dispose.assert_awaited_once()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_lifespan_does_not_require_telegram_client() -> None:
    """Authentication engine lifecycle remains valid without Academy Telegram."""

    application = MagicMock()

    scheduler_runtime = AsyncMock()
    auth_engine = AsyncMock()

    application.state.scheduler_runtime = scheduler_runtime
    application.state.auth_engine = auth_engine

    del application.state.announcement_telegram_client

    async with lifespan(application):
        pass

    scheduler_runtime.start.assert_awaited_once()
    scheduler_runtime.stop.assert_awaited_once()
    auth_engine.dispose.assert_awaited_once()

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.project_control_center.application.service import (
    ProjectControlService,
)
from app.project_control_center.domain.models import (
    ActionSnapshot,
    ActionStatus,
    IssueSnapshot,
    IssueStatus,
    MetricSnapshot,
    MilestoneSnapshot,
    ProjectDefinition,
    RiskSnapshot,
    Severity,
    SourceSnapshot,
    SourceStatus,
    WorkstreamSnapshot,
    WorkstreamStatus,
)
from app.project_control_center.domain.ports import ProjectStatusSource
from app.project_control_center.infrastructure.in_memory import (
    InMemoryProjectRegistry,
    InMemorySnapshotRepository,
)


@dataclass
class MixedSource(ProjectStatusSource):
    """
    Test source supporting both synchronous and asynchronous
    source methods.

    This represents the compatibility requirement of the
    asynchronous composition path.
    """

    @property
    def source_id(self) -> str:
        return "mixed"

    @property
    def source_name(self) -> str:
        return "Mixed Source"

    def health(self) -> SourceSnapshot:
        return SourceSnapshot(
            source_id=self.source_id,
            name=self.source_name,
            status=SourceStatus.AVAILABLE,
            updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

    def fetch_workstreams(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[WorkstreamSnapshot]:
        return [
            WorkstreamSnapshot(
                workstream_id="engineering",
                name="Engineering",
                completion_percent=90,
                status=WorkstreamStatus.HEALTHY,
                source=self.source_id,
            )
        ]

    async def fetch_metrics(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MetricSnapshot]:
        return [
            MetricSnapshot(
                key="tests",
                label="Tests",
                value=955,
                source=self.source_id,
            )
        ]

    async def fetch_issues(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[IssueSnapshot]:
        return [
            IssueSnapshot(
                issue_id="ISS-1",
                project_id=project_id,
                title="High severity issue",
                severity=Severity.HIGH,
                status=IssueStatus.OPEN,
                source=self.source_id,
            )
        ]

    async def fetch_risks(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[RiskSnapshot]:
        return [
            RiskSnapshot(
                risk_id="RISK-1",
                project_id=project_id,
                title="Governance risk",
                severity=Severity.HIGH,
                status="PENDING_DPO_GRC",
                source=self.source_id,
            )
        ]

    def fetch_actions(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[ActionSnapshot]:
        return []

    def fetch_milestones(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MilestoneSnapshot]:
        return []


class SyncSource(ProjectStatusSource):
    """
    Fully synchronous source used to verify that the existing
    synchronous composition path remains intact.
    """

    @property
    def source_id(self) -> str:
        return "sync-test"

    @property
    def source_name(self) -> str:
        return "Synchronous Test Source"

    def health(self) -> SourceSnapshot:
        return SourceSnapshot(
            source_id=self.source_id,
            name=self.source_name,
            status=SourceStatus.AVAILABLE,
            updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

    def fetch_workstreams(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[WorkstreamSnapshot]:
        return [
            WorkstreamSnapshot(
                workstream_id="engineering",
                name="Engineering",
                completion_percent=90,
                status=WorkstreamStatus.HEALTHY,
                source=self.source_id,
            )
        ]

    def fetch_metrics(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MetricSnapshot]:
        return [
            MetricSnapshot(
                key="tests",
                label="Tests",
                value=955,
                source=self.source_id,
            )
        ]

    def fetch_issues(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[IssueSnapshot]:
        return []

    def fetch_risks(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[RiskSnapshot]:
        return []

    def fetch_actions(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[ActionSnapshot]:
        return []

    def fetch_milestones(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MilestoneSnapshot]:
        return []


def make_service(
    source: ProjectStatusSource,
) -> ProjectControlService:
    registry = InMemoryProjectRegistry()
    snapshots = InMemorySnapshotRepository()

    registry.save(
        ProjectDefinition(
            project_id="easychatbot",
            name="EasyChatBot",
        )
    )

    return ProjectControlService(
        registry=registry,
        sources=(source,),
        snapshots=snapshots,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_async_composition_builds_snapshot():
    service = make_service(MixedSource())

    snapshot = await service.get_snapshot_async(
        "easychatbot",
        persist=False,
    )

    assert snapshot.project_id == "easychatbot"
    assert snapshot.project_name == "EasyChatBot"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_async_composition_aggregates_all_source_data():
    service = make_service(MixedSource())

    snapshot = await service.get_snapshot_async(
        "easychatbot",
        persist=False,
    )

    assert len(snapshot.workstreams) == 1
    assert len(snapshot.metrics) == 1
    assert len(snapshot.issues) == 1
    assert len(snapshot.risks) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_async_composition_preserves_existing_status_rules():
    service = make_service(MixedSource())

    snapshot = await service.get_snapshot_async(
        "easychatbot",
        persist=False,
    )

    assert snapshot.project_status.value == "blocked"
    assert snapshot.open_blockers == 1
    assert snapshot.open_risks == 1
    assert snapshot.release_readiness == "NOT READY"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_async_composition_persists_snapshot():
    service = make_service(MixedSource())

    snapshot = await service.get_snapshot_async(
        "easychatbot",
    )

    stored = service.snapshots.list_for_project(
        "easychatbot"
    )

    assert stored
    assert stored[-1] == snapshot


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_unknown_project_is_rejected():
    service = make_service(MixedSource())

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Unknown project: missing",
    ):
        await service.get_snapshot_async(
            "missing",
            persist=False,
        )


def test_existing_sync_composition_remains_unchanged():
    service = make_service(SyncSource())

    snapshot = service.get_snapshot(
        "easychatbot",
        persist=False,
    )

    assert snapshot.project_id == "easychatbot"
    assert snapshot.project_name == "EasyChatBot"
    assert snapshot.overall_progress_percent == 90
    assert snapshot.project_status.value == "on_track"

@dataclass
class FakeProductionHealthService:

    registry.save(
        ProjectDefinition(
            project_id="easychatbot",
            name="EasyChatBot",
        )

    snapshots = InMemorySnapshotRepository()

    )

    service = create_project_control_service(

        "production-health",

        "privacy-governance",
    ]

    (
        registry,

    assert service.sources[1]._dashboard_service is dashboard_service

@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "argument",
    [
        "registry",
        "snapshots",
        "health_service",
        "dashboard_service",
        "case_registry",
    ],
)
def test_factory_rejects_missing_required_dependency(argument):
    (
        registry,
        snapshots,
        health_service,
        dashboard_service,
        case_registry,
    ) = make_dependencies()

    dependencies = {
        "registry": registry,
        "snapshots": snapshots,
        "health_service": health_service,
        "dashboard_service": dashboard_service,
        "case_registry": case_registry,
    }

    dependencies[argument] = None

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=f"{argument} cannot be None",
    ):
        create_project_control_service(
            **dependencies,
        )


def test_factory_does_not_create_duplicate_authoritative_stores():
    (
        registry,
        snapshots,
        health_service,
        dashboard_service,
        case_registry,
    ) = make_dependencies()

    service = create_project_control_service(
        registry=registry,
        snapshots=snapshots,
        health_service=health_service,
        dashboard_service=dashboard_service,
        case_registry=case_registry,
    )

    assert service.sources[0]._health_service is health_service
    assert service.sources[1]._dashboard_service is dashboard_service
    assert service.sources[2]._case_registry is case_registry

    assert not hasattr(
        service,
        "health_state",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        service,
        "operations_state",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        service,
        "privacy_state",
    )

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.privacy.privacy_governance_case import (
    PrivacyGovernanceCase,
    PrivacyTriggerType,
)
from app.application.privacy.privacy_governance_case_registry import (
    PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry,
)
from app.project_control_center.domain.models import (
    Severity,
    SourceStatus,
)
from app.project_control_center.infrastructure.governance_risk_source import (
    GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource,
)


def make_case(
    *,
    case_id: str,
    risk: str,
    governance_status: str = "PENDING_DPO_GRC",
) -> PrivacyGovernanceCase:
    return PrivacyGovernanceCase(
        case_id=case_id,
        title=f"Privacy case {case_id}",
        summary="Test privacy governance case",
        trigger_type=PrivacyTriggerType.NEW_PROCESSING,
        triggered_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        trigger_source="test",
        change_id=f"CHANGE-{case_id}",
        risk=risk,
        governance_status=governance_status,
    )


def test_source_identity() -> None:
    registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry()

    source = GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
        case_registry=registry,
    )

    assert source.source_id == "privacy-governance"
    assert source.source_name == "Privacy Governance"


def test_health_reports_available() -> None:
    registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry()

    source = GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
        case_registry=registry,
    )

    result = source.health()

    assert result.source_id == "privacy-governance"
    assert result.name == "Privacy Governance"
    assert result.status is SourceStatus.AVAILABLE


def test_fetch_risks_maps_privacy_case_risk() -> None:
    registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry(
        [
            make_case(
                case_id="CASE-LOW",
                risk="low",
            ),
            make_case(
                case_id="CASE-MEDIUM",
                risk="medium",
            ),
            make_case(
                case_id="CASE-HIGH",
                risk="high",
            ),
        ]
    )

    source = GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
        case_registry=registry,
    )

    risks = source.fetch_risks("easychatbot")

    assert len(risks) == 3

    by_id = {
        risk.risk_id: risk
        for risk in risks
    }

    assert by_id["CASE-LOW"].severity is Severity.LOW
    assert by_id["CASE-MEDIUM"].severity is Severity.MEDIUM
    assert by_id["CASE-HIGH"].severity is Severity.HIGH


def test_fetch_risks_preserves_governance_status_separately() -> None:
    registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry(
        [
            make_case(
                case_id="CASE-1",
                risk="high",
                governance_status="PENDING_DPO_GRC",
            ),
            make_case(
                case_id="CASE-2",
                risk="high",
                governance_status="APPROVED",
            ),
            make_case(
                case_id="CASE-3",
                risk="high",
                governance_status="BLOCKED",
            ),
        ]
    )

    source = GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
        case_registry=registry,
    )

    risks = source.fetch_risks("easychatbot")

    by_id = {
        risk.risk_id: risk
        for risk in risks
    }

    assert by_id["CASE-1"].severity is Severity.HIGH
    assert by_id["CASE-1"].status == "PENDING_DPO_GRC"

    assert by_id["CASE-2"].severity is Severity.HIGH
    assert by_id["CASE-2"].status == "APPROVED"

    assert by_id["CASE-3"].severity is Severity.HIGH
    assert by_id["CASE-3"].status == "BLOCKED"


def test_fetch_risks_preserves_project_and_source_identity() -> None:
    registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry(
        [
            make_case(
                case_id="CASE-TRACE",
                risk="medium",
            )
        ]
    )

    source = GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
        case_registry=registry,
    )

    risks = source.fetch_risks("project-123")

    assert len(risks) == 1
    risk = risks[0]

    assert risk.project_id == "project-123"
    assert risk.risk_id == "CASE-TRACE"
    assert risk.title == "Privacy case CASE-TRACE"
    assert risk.source == "privacy-governance"
    assert risk.workstream_id is None
    assert risk.owner is None


def test_fetch_risks_is_read_only() -> None:
    case = make_case(
        case_id="CASE-READONLY",
        risk="high",
    )

    registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry([case])

    source = GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
        case_registry=registry,
    )

    before = registry.all()

    source.fetch_risks("easychatbot")

    after = registry.all()

    assert after == before
    assert after[0] == case


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "method_name",
    [
        "fetch_workstreams",
        "fetch_metrics",
        "fetch_issues",
        "fetch_actions",
        "fetch_milestones",
    ],
)
def test_non_risk_project_contracts_remain_empty(
    method_name: str,
) -> None:
    registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry()

    source = GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
        case_registry=registry,
    )

    method = getattr(source, method_name)

    assert method("easychatbot") == []


def test_unknown_risk_fails_closed_to_medium() -> None:
    registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry(
        [
            make_case(
                case_id="CASE-UNKNOWN",
                risk="unexpected-risk",
            )
        ]
    )

    source = GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
        case_registry=registry,
    )

    risks = source.fetch_risks("easychatbot")

    assert len(risks) == 1
    assert risks[0].severity is Severity.MEDIUM


def test_none_registry_is_rejected() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="case_registry cannot be None"):
        GovernanceRiskProjectStatusSource(
            case_registry=None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from app.project_control_center.domain.models import ProjectDefinition
from app.project_control_center.infrastructure.in_memory import (
    InMemoryProjectRegistry,
)


def create_production_project_registry() -> InMemoryProjectRegistry:
    """Build the Project Control registry used by production composition."""
    registry = InMemoryProjectRegistry()

    registry.save(
        ProjectDefinition(
            project_id="easychatbot",
            name="EasyChatBot",
        )
    )

    return registry


def test_production_project_registry_registers_easychatbot():
    """Protect the production bootstrap registration of the EasyChatBot project."""
    registry = create_production_project_registry()

    project = registry.get("easychatbot")

    assert project is not None
    assert project.project_id == "easychatbot"
    assert project.name == "EasyChatBot"


def test_production_project_registry_does_not_require_duplicate_registration():
    """Verify the canonical project can be resolved from the production registry."""
    registry = create_production_project_registry()

    project = registry.get("easychatbot")

    assert isinstance(project, ProjectDefinition)
    assert project.project_id == "easychatbot"

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\unit\operations_center\email_workflows\test_registry_bootstrap.py

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.domain import (
    EmailWorkflowLifecycle,
)
from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.in_memory_registry import (
    InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry,
)
from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.registry_bootstrap import (
    EmailWorkflowRegistryBootstrap,
)


def test_bootstrap_creates_validated_built_in_workflows():
    registry = InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry()

    result = EmailWorkflowRegistryBootstrap(registry).ensure_registered(
        now=datetime(2026, 9, 18, 20, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )

    assert result.created == (
        "cybersecurity_daily_report",
        "cybersecurity_weekly_report",
        "cybersecurity_critical_alert",
        "platform_intelligence_escalation",
    )

    assert (
        registry.get("cybersecurity_daily_report").schedule
        == "daily_08:00"
    )
    assert (
        registry.get("cybersecurity_weekly_report").schedule
        == "weekly_monday_08:00"
    )
    assert (
        registry.get("cybersecurity_critical_alert").schedule
        is None
    )

    platform_intelligence = registry.get(
        "platform_intelligence_escalation"
    )

    assert platform_intelligence is not None
    assert platform_intelligence.schedule is None
    assert (
        platform_intelligence.lifecycle
        is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE
    )
    assert platform_intelligence.owner == "platform_intelligence"
    assert platform_intelligence.trigger == "escalation_recommendation"
    assert (
        platform_intelligence.template_id
        == "platform_intelligence_escalation"
    )

    assert (
        registry.get("cybersecurity_daily_report").lifecycle
        is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE
    )


def test_bootstrap_is_idempotent_and_does_not_overwrite():
    registry = InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry()
    bootstrap = EmailWorkflowRegistryBootstrap(registry)

    first = bootstrap.ensure_registered(
        now=datetime(2026, 9, 18, 20, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )

    before = registry.get("cybersecurity_daily_report")

    second = bootstrap.ensure_registered(
        now=datetime(2026, 9, 19, 20, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )

    after = registry.get("cybersecurity_daily_report")

    assert len(first.created) == 4
    assert second.created == ()
    assert len(second.already_present) == 4
    assert before == after


def test_bootstrap_rejects_naive_clock():
    registry = InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timezone-aware"):
        EmailWorkflowRegistryBootstrap(registry).ensure_registered(
            now=datetime(2026, 9, 18, 20, 0)
        )

------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\unit\operations_center\test_platform_intelligence_escalation_retry.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus,
)


REFERENCE = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    0,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = REFERENCE
    updated_at: datetime | None = REFERENCE


def policy(**overrides):
    values = {
        "policy_id": "PI-RETRY",
        "policy_version": "1.0",
        "status": (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
            .APPROVED
        ),
        "max_attempts": 4,
        "base_backoff_seconds": 60,
        "max_backoff_seconds": 300,
        "retryable_error_codes": frozenset(
            {
                "TIMEOUT",
                "CONNECTION_ERROR",
                "PROVIDER_ERROR",
            }
        ),
        "retry_interrupted_started": True,
    }

    values.update(overrides)

    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy(
        **values
    )


def record(
    *,
    status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
    attempt=1,
    error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
):
    return Record(
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=attempt,
        status=status,
        error_code=error_code,
        created_at=REFERENCE,
        updated_at=REFERENCE,
    )


def test_retryable_failed_delivery_is_eligible_with_deterministic_backoff():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=1),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )

    assert result.policy_id == "PI-RETRY"
    assert result.policy_version == "1.0"


def test_failed_delivery_is_not_due_before_backoff():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=1),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=59),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )


def test_backoff_doubles_and_is_capped():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=1) == 60
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=2) == 120
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=3) == 240
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=4) == 300
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=10) == 300


def test_non_retryable_error_is_not_eligible():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            error_code="INVALID_RECIPIENT"

        reference_at=REFERENCE,

        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE

    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()

        ValueError,

                22,
                10,
                0,
            ),
        )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="max_backoff_seconds",
    ):
        policy(
            base_backoff_seconds=300,
            max_backoff_seconds=60,
        )

    with pytest.raises(

        policy(
            max_attempts=0
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidate_discovery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService,
)

T1 = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
T2 = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 10, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = T1
    updated_at: datetime | None = T1


class Repository:
    def __init__(self, records):
        self.records = records
        self.limit = None

    async def list_retry_candidates(self, *, limit):
        self.limit = limit
        return self.records


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_returns_only_failed_and_started_candidates():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-succeeded",
                "rec-s",
                "dec-s",
                "rev-s",
                "corr-s",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-failed",
                "rec-f",
                "dec-f",
                "rev-f",
                "corr-f",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
                updated_at=T2,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-started",
                "rec-t",
                "dec-t",
                "rev-t",
                "corr-t",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=50)

    assert repository.limit == 50
    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.candidates] == [
        "key-started",
        "key-failed",
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_is_deterministically_ordered():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-b",
                "rec-b",
                "dec-b",
                "rev-b",
                "corr-b",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-a",
                "rec-a",
                "dec-a",
                "rev-a",
                "corr-a",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=10)

    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.candidates] == [
        "key-a",
        "key-b",
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_preserves_retry_metadata():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-1",
                "rec-1",
                "dec-1",
                "rev-1",
                "corr-1",
                2,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                error_code="TIMEOUT",
                updated_at=T2,
            )
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=10)
    candidate = result.candidates[0]

    assert candidate.attempt == 2
    assert candidate.error_code == "TIMEOUT"
    assert candidate.updated_at == T2
    assert candidate.status == "FAILED"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_rejects_invalid_limit():
    repository = Repository([])

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least 1"):
        await service.discover(limit=0)

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claims import (
    RetryCandidateClaimStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claim_service import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService,
)


class FakeClaimRepository:
    def __init__(self, result: bool) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls = []

    async def claim_retry_candidate(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(kwargs)
        return self.result


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_returns_bounded_lease() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(True)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(
        repository,
        lease_seconds=60,
    )
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    claim = await service.claim(
        notification_key="pi-key",
        claimed_at=reference,
    )

    assert claim is not None
    assert claim.notification_key == "pi-key"
    assert claim.claimed_at == reference
    assert claim.lease_expires_at == reference + timedelta(seconds=60)
    assert claim.status is RetryCandidateClaimStatus.CLAIMED
    assert repository.calls[0]["notification_key"] == "pi-key"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_returns_none_when_candidate_is_already_claimed() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(False)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(repository)

    claim = await service.claim(
        notification_key="pi-key",
        claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )

    assert claim is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_rejects_naive_reference_time() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(True)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(repository)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timezone-aware"):
        await service.claim(
            notification_key="pi-key",
            claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0),
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus,
)


DELIVERY_TIME = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    0,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)

REFERENCE = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    1,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = DELIVERY_TIME
    updated_at: datetime | None = DELIVERY_TIME


class Repository:
    def __init__(self, record):
        self.record = record
        self.retries = 0

    async def get_by_notification_key(self, key):
        if self.record is None:
            return None

        if self.record.notification_key == key:
            return self.record

        return None

    async def start_retry(self, *, notification_key):
        self.retries += 1

        self.record.attempt += 1
        self.record.status = (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        )
        self.record.error_code = None
        self.record.updated_at = REFERENCE

        return self.record


class Dispatch:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = 0

    async def dispatch(self, *, request, email_request):
        self.calls += 1

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchStatus.SENT,
            notification_key=request.notification_key,
            provider_message_id="provider-1",
            attempt=2,
        )


def policy():
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy(
            policy_id="PI-RETRY",
            policy_version="1.0",
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
                .APPROVED
            ),
            max_attempts=4,
            base_backoff_seconds=60,
            max_backoff_seconds=300,
            retryable_error_codes=frozenset(
                {"PROVIDER_ERROR"}
            ),
            retry_interrupted_started=True,
        )
    )


def request():
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("High risk.",),
    )


def record(
    status,
    attempt=1,
    error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
):
    return Record(
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=attempt,
        status=status,
        error_code=error_code,
        created_at=DELIVERY_TIME,
        updated_at=DELIVERY_TIME,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_failed_delivery_retries_only_when_due():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    early = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=datetime(
            2026,
            9,
            22,
            10,
            0,
            30,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    assert (
        early.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_DUE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0

    due = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        due.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .RETRIED
    )

    assert repository.retries == 1
    assert dispatch.calls == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_non_retryable_failure_does_not_dispatch():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
            error_code="INVALID_RECIPIENT",
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    result = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_started_recovery_does_not_increment_attempt():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
            error_code=None,
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    result = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .RETRIED
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert repository.record.attempt == 1
    assert dispatch.calls == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_max_attempts_prevents_retry():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
            attempt=4,
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    result = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retry_requires_existing_durable_record():
    repository = Repository(None)
    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="existing durable",
    ):
        await service.retry(
            request=request(),
            email_request=object(),
            reference_at=REFERENCE,
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidates import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryState,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestrator import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator,
)


REFERENCE = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

NOTIFICATION_KEY = (
    "platform_intelligence_escalation:recommendation-1"
)


def dispatch_request() -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest:
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        review_item_id="review-1",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        correlation_id="correlation-1",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("High risk level.",),
    )


def candidate() -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate:
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate(
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        review_item_id="review-1",
        correlation_id="correlation-1",
        attempt=1,
        status="FAILED",
        updated_at=REFERENCE,
        error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
    )


class FakePolicy:
    def __init__(self, eligible: bool) -> None:
        self.eligible = eligible
        self.calls = 0

    async def evaluate(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.eligible


class FakeClaimService:
    def __init__(self, claim) -> None:
        self.claim_result = claim
        self.calls = 0

    async def claim(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.claim_result


class FakeStateReader:
    def __init__(self, state) -> None:
        self.state = state
        self.calls = 0

    async def get_retry_state(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.state


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FakeClaim:
    token: str = "claim-token"


class FakeRetryExecution:
    def __init__(
        self,
        result: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls = 0
        self.kwargs = None

    async def retry(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        self.kwargs = kwargs
        return self.result


def valid_state() -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryState:
    return PlatformIntelligenceRetryState(
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        recommendation_status="RECOMMENDED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        dispatch_request=dispatch_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_not_policy_eligible_does_not_claim_or_execute() -> None:
    policy = FakePolicy(False)
    claims = FakeClaimService(FakeClaim())
    state = FakeStateReader(valid_state())

    execution = FakeRetryExecution(
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
            attempt=1,
            reason="SUCCEEDED",
        )
    )

    orchestrator = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator(
        state_reader=state,
        policy_evaluator=policy,
        claim_service=claims,
        retry_execution=execution,
    )

    result = await orchestrator.run_candidate(
        candidate=candidate(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.reason == "RETRY_POLICY_NOT_ELIGIBLE"
    assert claims.calls == 0
    assert state.calls == 0
    assert execution.calls == 0

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidates import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker_service import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker,
)


REFERENCE = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


class Candidate:
    def __init__(self, key: str, attempt: int = 1) -> None:
        self.notification_key = key
        self.recommendation_id = f"rec-{key}"
        self.attempt = attempt


class Discovery:
    def __init__(self, candidates) -> None:
        self.candidates = tuple(candidates)
        self.calls = 0
        self.limit = None

    async def discover(self, *, limit: int):
        self.calls += 1
        self.limit = limit
        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch(
            candidates=self.candidates,
            limit=limit,
        )


class Orchestrator:
    def __init__(self, results=None, errors=None) -> None:
        self.results = results or {}
        self.errors = errors or {}
        self.calls = []

    async def run_candidate(self, *, candidate, reference_at):
        self.calls.append((candidate.notification_key, reference_at))
        if candidate.notification_key in self.errors:
            raise self.errors[candidate.notification_key]
        return self.results[candidate.notification_key]


def run_result(key, status, reason, attempt=1, provider=None):
    return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
        notification_key=key,
        status=status,
        reason=reason,
        attempt=attempt,
        provider_message_id=provider,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_batch_is_a_noop():
    discovery = Discovery([])
    orchestrator = Orchestrator()

    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=discovery,
        orchestrator=orchestrator,
    )

    result = await worker.run(
        request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=10),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.discovered_count == 0
    assert result.processed_count == 0
    assert result.succeeded_count == 0
    assert result.skipped_count == 0
    assert result.failed_count == 0
    assert result.items == ()
    assert discovery.calls == 1
    assert discovery.limit == 10
    assert orchestrator.calls == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_worker_processes_bounded_batch_and_preserves_results():
    candidates = [Candidate("key-a"), Candidate("key-b"), Candidate("key-c")]
    discovery = Discovery(candidates)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator(
        results={
            "key-a": run_result(
                "key-a",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                "RETRY_DISPATCHED",
                attempt=2,
                provider="provider-a",
            ),
            "key-b": run_result(
                "key-b",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                "RETRY_POLICY_NOT_ELIGIBLE",
            ),
            "key-c": run_result(
                "key-c",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.FAILED,
                "DISPATCH_FAILED",
            ),
        }
    )

    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=discovery,
        orchestrator=orchestrator,
    )

    result = await worker.run(
        request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=3),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.discovered_count == 3
    assert result.processed_count == 3
    assert result.succeeded_count == 1
    assert result.skipped_count == 1
    assert result.failed_count == 1
    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.items] == [
        "key-a",
        "key-b",
        "key-c",
    ]
    assert result.items[0].status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SUCCEEDED
    assert result.items[0].provider_message_id == "provider-a"
    assert result.items[1].status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.items[2].status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.FAILED
    assert orchestrator.calls == [
        ("key-a", REFERENCE),
        ("key-b", REFERENCE),
        ("key-c", REFERENCE),
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_one_candidate_exception_does_not_abort_remaining_candidates():
    candidates = [Candidate("key-a"), Candidate("key-b"), Candidate("key-c")]
    discovery = Discovery(candidates)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator(
        results={
            "key-a": run_result(
                "key-a",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                "RETRY_DISPATCHED",
            ),
            "key-c": run_result(
                "key-c",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                "RETRY_CANDIDATE_ALREADY_CLAIMED",
            ),
        },
        errors={"key-b": RuntimeError("provider failure")},
    )

    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=discovery,
        orchestrator=orchestrator,
    )

    result = await worker.run(
        request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=3),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.processed_count == 3
    assert result.succeeded_count == 1
    assert result.skipped_count == 1
    assert result.failed_count == 1

    failed = result.items[1]
    assert failed.notification_key == "key-b"
    assert failed.reason == "CANDIDATE_EXECUTION_ERROR"
    assert failed.error_type == "RuntimeError"

    assert len(orchestrator.calls) == 3


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_worker_rejects_naive_reference_time():
    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=Discovery([]),
        orchestrator=Orchestrator(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timezone-aware"):
        await worker.run(
            request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=1),
            reference_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0),
        )


def test_worker_request_rejects_invalid_limit():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least 1"):
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=0)


def test_worker_result_rejects_duplicate_notification_keys():
    from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker import (
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult,
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult,
    )

    item = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
        notification_key="duplicate",
        status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SKIPPED,
        reason="SKIPPED",
        attempt=1,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate"):
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult(
            requested_limit=2,
            discovered_count=2,
            processed_count=2,
            succeeded_count=0,
            skipped_count=2,
            failed_count=0,
            items=(item, item),
        )
